\displaystyle|(Q^{+}(f,g),h\langle v\rangle^{2k})|\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*}^{\prime})||g(v^{\prime})||h(v)|\langle v\rangle^{2k}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k-6+\gamma}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{6}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{6}|f(v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}|g(v^{\prime})|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{2\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\frac{\langle v\rangle^{6}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{6}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{6}}\langle v\rangle^{2k-\gamma}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2},

Since
-3<2\gamma\leq 2
, by Lemma
2.11
we have

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{2\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\frac{\langle v\rangle^{6}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{6}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{6}}\langle v\rangle^{2k-\gamma}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\leq C_{k}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2}.

Since
2k-6+\gamma\geq 0
, by pre-post collisional change of variables we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k-6+\gamma}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{6}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{6}|f(v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}|g(v^{\prime})|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2k-6+\gamma}\langle v\rangle^{6}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{6}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k+\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{6}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{6}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{2k+\gamma}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L^{2}_{3}}^{2}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2}+\|g\|_{L^{2}_{3}}^{2}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2},

so the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
is proved. For the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{5}{2},-\frac{3}{2}]
, we have
-3<\gamma-\frac{1}{2}<0,-2<\gamma+\frac{1}{2}<0
, hence

\displaystyle|(Q^{+}(f,g),h\langle v\rangle^{2k})|\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*}^{\prime})||g(v^{\prime})||h(v)|\langle v\rangle^{2k}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+\frac{1}{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k-\frac{9}{2}}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{4}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{4}|f(v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}|g(v^{\prime})|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma-\frac{1}{2}}b(\cos\theta)\frac{\langle v\rangle^{4}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{4}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{4}}\langle v\rangle^{2k+\frac{1}{2}}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2},

still by Lemma
2.11
we have

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma-\frac{1}{2}}b(\cos\theta)\frac{\langle v\rangle^{4}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{4}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{4}}\langle v\rangle^{2k+\frac{1}{2}}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\leq C_{k}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2},

similarly we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+\frac{1}{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2k-\frac{9}{2}}\langle v\rangle^{4}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+\frac{1}{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k-\frac{1}{2}}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+\frac{1}{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{4}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{2k-\frac{1}{2}}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma.:=I_{1}+I_{2}.

Since
\gamma+\frac{1}{2}\in(-2,0)
by Lemma
2.6
we have

I_{1}\lesssim\min_{m+n=1}\{\|f\|_{H^{m}_{4}}^{2}\|g\|_{H^{n}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2}\},\quad I_{2}\lesssim\min_{m+n=1}\{\|f\|_{H^{m}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2}\|g\|_{H^{n}_{4}}^{2}\},

so the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{5}{2},-\frac{3}{2}]
is proved. For the case
\gamma\in(-3,0)
we have

\displaystyle|(Q^{+}(f,g),h\langle v\rangle^{2k})|\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*}^{\prime})||g(v^{\prime})||h(v)|\langle v\rangle^{2k}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k-4}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{4}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{4}|f(v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}|g(v^{\prime})|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\frac{\langle v\rangle^{4}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{4}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{4}}\langle v\rangle^{2k}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2},

still by Lemma
2.11
we have

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\frac{\langle v\rangle^{4}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{4}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{4}}\langle v\rangle^{2k}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\leq C_{k}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2},

and by pre-post collisional change of variables we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2k-4}\langle v\rangle^{4}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{4}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{2k}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma:=I_{1}+I_{2}.

Since
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{5}{2}]
by Lemma
2.5
we have

I_{1}\lesssim\min_{m+n=2}\{\|f\|_{H^{m}_{4}}^{2}\|g\|_{H^{n}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2}\},\quad I_{2}\lesssim\min_{m+n=2}\{\|f\|_{H^{m}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2}\|g\|_{H^{n}_{4}}^{2}\},

thus the theorem is proved by gathering all the terms together.
∎

The estimate for the
Q^{-}
term is similar.

Lemma 3.4
.

For any
\gamma\in(-3,1]
, for smooth function
f,g,h
, recall
N
is defined in (
8
), for any
k\geq 4
we have

|(Q^{-}(f,g),h\langle v\rangle^{2k})|\leq C_{k}\min_{m+n=N-2}\{\|f\|_{H^{m}_{3}}\|g\|_{H^{n}_{k+\gamma/2}}\}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}},

where
m,n
are nonnegative integers.

Proof.

The proof is similar to the
Q^{+}
case, first for the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
we have

\displaystyle|(Q^{-}(f,g),h\langle v\rangle^{2k})|\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*})||g(v)||h(v)|\langle v\rangle^{2k}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k+\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{6}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{2\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\frac{1}{\langle v_{*}\rangle^{6}}\langle v\rangle^{2k-\gamma}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2},

it is easily seen that

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k+\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{6}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\leq C_{k}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{3}}^{2}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2},

since
2\gamma\in(-3,2]
we have

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{2\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\frac{1}{\langle v_{*}\rangle^{6}}\langle v\rangle^{2k-\gamma}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\leq C_{k}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2},

so the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
is thus proved. For the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{5}{2},-\frac{3}{2}]
, we have
-3<\gamma-\frac{1}{2}<0,-2<\gamma+\frac{1}{2}<0
, hence

\displaystyle|(Q^{-}(f,g),h\langle v\rangle^{2k})|\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+\frac{1}{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k-\frac{1}{2}}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma-\frac{1}{2}}b(\cos\theta)\frac{1}{\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}}\langle v\rangle^{2k+\frac{1}{2}}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2},

since
-3<\gamma-\frac{1}{2}<0
, it is easily seen that

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma-\frac{1}{2}}b(\cos\theta)\frac{1}{\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}}\langle v\rangle^{2k+\frac{1}{2}}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\leq C_{k}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2},

since
-2<\gamma-\frac{1}{2}<0
, by Lemma
2.6
we have

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma+\frac{1}{2}}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k-\frac{1}{2}}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\lesssim\min_{m+n=1}\{\|f\|_{H^{m}_{4}}^{2}\|g\|_{H^{n}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2}\},

so the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{5}{2},-\frac{3}{2}]
is proved. For the case
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{5}{2}]
we have

\displaystyle|(Q^{-}(f,g),h\langle v\rangle^{2k})|\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\frac{1}{\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}}\langle v\rangle^{2k}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2},

it is easily seen that

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\frac{1}{\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}}\langle v\rangle^{2k}|h(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\leq C_{k}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2},

and by Lemma
2.6

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{2k}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{4}|f(v_{*})|^{2}|g(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\lesssim\min_{m+n=2}\{\|f\|_{H^{m}_{4}}^{2}\|g\|_{H^{n}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2}\},

the lemma is proved by gathering all the terms together.
∎

Corollary 3.5
.

For any
\gamma\in(-3,1]
, for smooth function
f,g,h
, for any
k\geq 4
we have

|(Q(f,g),h\langle v\rangle^{2k})|\leq C_{k}\min_{m+n=N-2}\{\|f\|_{H^{m}_{k+\gamma/2}}\|g\|_{H^{n}_{4}}+\|g\|_{H^{m}_{k+\gamma/2}}\|f\|_{H^{n}_{4}}\}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}},

with
m,n
nonnegative integers.

Proof.

By taking
m=m_{1},n=n_{1}
in Lemma
3.3
and Lemma
3.4
the proof is finished.
∎

Corollary 3.6
.

For any
-3<\gamma\leq 1
, for any
|\beta|\leq 4
, for any
f,g
smooth we have

|(Q(f,\partial_{\beta}\mu),g\langle v\rangle^{2k})|+|(Q(\partial_{\beta}\mu,f),g\langle v\rangle^{2k})|\leq C_{k}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}},

for some constant
C_{k}>0
.

Proof.

By taking
m=n_{1}=0
in Lemma
3.3
and Lemma
3.4
we have

\displaystyle|(Q(f,\partial_{\beta}\mu),g\langle v\rangle^{2k})|+|(Q(\partial_{\beta}\mu,f),g\langle v\rangle^{2k})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}\|\partial_{\beta}\mu\|_{H^{n}_{5}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}+C_{k}\|\partial_{\beta}\mu\|_{H^{m_{1}}_{k+\gamma/2}}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{5}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}\leq C_{k}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}},

so the proof is ended.
∎

The estimate for the exponential weight case is similar.

Lemma 3.7
.

For any
\gamma\in(-3,1]
, for smooth function
f,g,h
, for any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},a>0,b\in(0,2)
we have

\displaystyle|(Q(f,g),h\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})|\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,a,b}\min_{m+n=N-2}\{\|f\|_{H^{m}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}\|g\|_{H^{n}_{k-96,a,b}}+\|g\|_{H^{m}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}\|f\|_{H^{n}_{k-96,a,b}}\}\|h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}},

with
m,n
nonnegative integers.

Proof.

For the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
, by Lemma
2.16
we have

\langle v\rangle^{k-100}e^{a\langle v\rangle^{b}}\leq C_{k,a,b}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k-100}e^{a\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{b}}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k-100}e^{a\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{b}},

which implies

\displaystyle|(Q^{+}(f,g),he^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}}\langle v\rangle^{2k})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*}^{\prime})||g(v^{\prime})||h(v)|\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,a,b}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*}^{\prime})|\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k-100}e^{a\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{b}}|g(v^{\prime})|\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k-100}e^{a\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{b}}|h(v)|\langle v\rangle^{k+100}e^{a\langle v\rangle^{b}}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(Q^{+}(f\langle v\rangle^{k-100}e^{a\langle v\rangle^{b}},g\langle v\rangle^{k-100}e^{a\langle v\rangle^{b}}),(h\langle v\rangle^{k-100}e^{a\langle v\rangle^{b}})\langle v\rangle^{200}),

taking
k=100
in Lemma
3.3
the
Q^{+}
part is proved. Similarly for the
Q^{-}
term we have

(Q^{-}(f,g),he^{2\alpha\langle v\rangle^{\beta}}\langle v\rangle^{2k})=(Q^{-}(f,g\langle v\rangle^{k-100}e^{\alpha\langle v\rangle^{\beta}}),(h\langle v\rangle^{k-100}e^{\alpha\langle v\rangle^{\beta}})\langle v\rangle^{200}),

we conclude by taking
k=100
in Corollary
3.5
.
∎

Corollary 3.8
.

For any
-3<\gamma\leq 1
, for any
|\beta|\leq 4
, for any
f,g
smooth we have

|(Q(f,\partial_{\beta}\mu),g\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{a\langle v\rangle^{b}})|+|(Q(\partial_{\beta}\mu,f),g\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{\alpha\langle v\rangle^{\beta}})|\leq C_{k,a,b}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}\|g\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}},

for some constant
C_{k,a,b}>0
.

Proof.

The proof is similar as Lemma
3.6
thus omitted.
∎

Next we come to prove the linearized part for the exponential weight case.

Lemma 3.9
.

For any
-3<\gamma\leq 1
, for any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},a>0,b\in(0,2)
and
f
smooth, we have

|(Q^{+}(\mu,f),f\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})|+|(Q^{+}(f,\mu),f\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})|\leq C_{k,a,b}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2-b(\gamma+3)/4,a,b}},

for some constant
C_{k}>0
.

Proof.

For the first term, by Lemma
2.16
we have

\langle v\rangle^{k}e^{\frac{1}{2}a\langle v\rangle^{b}}\lesssim\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k}e^{\frac{1}{2}a\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{b}}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}e^{\frac{1}{2}a\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{b}},

together with Lemma
2.12
we have

\displaystyle|(Q^{+}(f,\mu),f\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|\mu(v_{*}^{\prime})||f(v^{\prime})||f(v)|\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)e^{-\frac{1}{2}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k}e^{\frac{1}{2}a\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{b}}|f(v^{\prime})|\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}e^{\frac{1}{2}a\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{b}}|f(v)|\langle v\rangle^{k}e^{\frac{3}{2}a\langle v\rangle^{b}}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)e^{-\frac{1}{2}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}e^{a\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{b}}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{b}}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2k}|f(v^{\prime})|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\frac{e^{a\langle v\rangle^{b}}}{e^{a\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{b}}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}}\langle v\rangle^{2k}|f(v)|^{2}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle C_{k,a,b}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2-b(\gamma+3)/4,a,b}},

the second term can be proved by changing
v^{\prime}
and
v_{*}^{\prime}
, so the proof is thus finished.
∎

Lemma 3.10
.

For any
-3<\gamma\leq 1
, for any
k\geq 0,a>0,b\in(0,2)
,
f
smooth, we have

-(Q^{-}(\mu,f),f\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})-(Q^{-}(f,\mu),f\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})\leq-C_{1}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}^{2}+C_{k,a,b}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{3}}^{2},

for some constants
C_{1},C_{k,a,b}>0
.

Proof.

It is esaily seen that

(-Q^{-}(\mu,f),f\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})=-\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}b(\cos\theta)d\sigma\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\mu(v_{*})|f|^{2}\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}}dvdv_{*},

and by Lemma
2.8
we have

\displaystyle|(Q^{-}(f,\mu),f\langle\cdot\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})|=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)f(v_{*})\mu(v)f(v)\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*})||f(v)|dvdv_{*}d\sigma\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}_{3}}^{2}.

Gathering the two terms, the lemma is thus proved.
∎

Corollary 3.11
.

For any
-3<\gamma\leq 1
, for any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},a>0,b\in(0,2)
and
f
smooth, we have

(Q(\mu,f),f\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})+(Q(f,\mu),f\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})\leq-C_{1}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}^{2}+C_{k,a,b}\|f\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

for some constants
C_{1},C_{k,a,b}>0
.

Lemma 3.12
.

For any
-3<\gamma\leq 1
,
f
smooth, define

\bar{L}f=-v\cdot\nabla_{x}f+Q(\mu,f)+Q(f,\mu),

then for any
k>4
we have

(32)

(\bar{L}f,f\langle v\rangle^{2k})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq-C\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2},

for some constants
C,C_{k}>0
. For any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},a>0,b\in(0,2)
we have

(33)

(\bar{L}f,f\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq-C_{1}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}^{2}+C_{k,a,b}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2},

for some constants
C_{1},C_{k,a,b}>0
. As a consequence for the solution to the inhomogeneous Boltzmann equation

\partial_{t}f=\bar{L}f=-v\cdot\nabla_{x}f+Q(f,\mu)+Q(\mu,f),\quad f|_{t=0}=f_{0}.

If
\gamma\in[0,1]
, for any
k>4
we have

\|f-Pf\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t}\|f_{0}-Pf_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}},

for some constant
\lambda>0
. If
\gamma\in(-3,0)
, for any
4<k_{0}<k
we have

\|f-Pf\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k_{0}}}\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{k-k_{*}}{|\gamma|}}\|f_{0}-Pf_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}},\quad\forall k_{*}\in(k_{0},k).

For the exponential weight, if
\gamma\in[0,1]
, for any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},0<a,b\in(0,2)
we have

\|f-Pf\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t}\|f_{0}-Pf_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}},

for some constant
\lambda>0
. If
\gamma\in(-3,0)
, for any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},0<a_{0}<a,b\in(0,2)
we have

\|f-Pf\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a_{0},b}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t^{\frac{b}{b-\gamma}}}\|f_{0}-Pf_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}},

for some constant
\lambda>0
.

Proof.

The exponential case (
33
) just follows from Corollary
3.11
above. We only prove the polynomial case (
32
).
By Lemma
3.1
and
3.2
we have

(Lf,f\langle v\rangle^{2k})=-\|b(\cos\theta)(\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}-\sin^{k-2}\frac{\theta}{2})\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,*}}+C_{k}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2-1/2}}^{2},

if
k>4
, then

\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}-\sin^{k-2}\frac{\theta}{2}\geq\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(1-\sin^{k-4}\frac{\theta}{2})>0,

so the polynomial case follows by interpolation. Then we come to prove the convergence rate. By combing the results in
[
47
,
46
,
38
,
22
]
we have

\|(I-P)S_{L}(t)f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-1/2})}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t}\|(I-P)S_{L}(t)f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-1/2})},\quad\hbox{if}\quad\gamma\geq 0,

and

\|(I-P)S_{L}(t)f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-1/2})}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t^{\frac{2}{2-\gamma}}}\|(I-P)S_{L}(t)f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-3/4})},\quad\hbox{if}\quad-3<\gamma<0.

Define two operators
L=A+B
with
A=M\chi_{R_{1}}
and
B=L-M\chi_{R_{1}}
for some
M,R_{1}>0
large, where
\chi_{R_{1}}
is the truncation function in ball with center zero and radius
R_{1}>0
. Denote
S_{L}
and
S_{B}
semigroups generated by
L
and
B
respectively. Then if
M,R_{1}
is large we have

(Bf,f)_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}\lesssim-\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}},\quad\|Af\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-3/4})}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}.

For the case
\gamma\geq 0
, for the polynomial case we have

\|S_{B}(t)f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}.

By Duhamel’s principle we have

\displaystyle\|(I-P)S_{L}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\|(I-P)S_{L}(t-s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-1/2})\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-1/2})}\|A\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-1/2})}\|S_{B}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}ds

\displaystyle+\|(I-P)S_{B}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t},

and for the exponential weight case the proof for
\gamma\geq 0
is the same. For the case
\gamma<0
, we first prove the polynomial case, on one hand, we have

\|S_{B}(t)f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}.

On the other hand, for any
k_{0}<k
, for any
R>0
we have

\frac{d}{dt}\|S_{B}(t)f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k_{0}}}\leq-c\|S_{B}(t)f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k_{0}+\gamma/2}}\leq-c\langle R\rangle^{\gamma}\|S_{B}(t)f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k_{0}}}+C\langle R\rangle^{-2(k-k_{0})+\gamma}\|S_{B}(t)f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}},

where we use the following interpolation

\langle R\rangle^{\gamma}\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k_{0}}}\leq\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k_{0}+\gamma/2}}+\langle R\rangle^{-2(k-k_{0})+\gamma}\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}.

Integrating the differential inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\|S_{B}(t)f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k_{0}}}\lesssim e^{-c\langle R\rangle^{\gamma}t}\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k_{0}}}+\langle R\rangle^{-2(k-k_{0})}\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\inf_{R>0}(e^{-c\langle R\rangle^{\gamma}t}+\langle R\rangle^{-2(k-k_{0})})\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{2(k-k_{*})}{|\gamma|}}\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}},\quad\forall k_{*}\in(k_{0},k),

where we choose
\langle R\rangle=\langle t\rangle^{-1/\gamma}[\log(1+t)^{-\frac{2}{c}(k-k_{0})}]^{1/\gamma}
. Moreover, thanks to Duhamel’s formula

\displaystyle\|(I-P)S_{L}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k_{0}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|(I-P)S_{B}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k_{0}}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\|(I-P)S_{L}(t-s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-3/4})\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-1/2})}\|A\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-3/4})}\|S_{B}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k_{0}}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{k-k_{*}}{|\gamma|}},\quad\forall k_{*}\in(k_{0},k),

so the proof for the polynomial case is thus finished. For the exponential weight case, if
M,R_{1}
is large we have

(Bf,f)_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}\leq-c\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}^{2},\quad\|Af\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-3/4})}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}.

So first we have

\|S_{B}(t)f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}.

On the other hand, for any
a_{0}<a
, for any constant
R>0
we have

\frac{d}{dt}\|S_{B}(t)f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a_{0},b}}^{2}\leq-c\|S_{B}(t)f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a_{0},b}}^{2}\leq-c\langle R\rangle^{\gamma}\|S_{B}(t)f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a_{0},b}}^{2}+\langle R\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-(a-a_{0})\langle R\rangle^{b}}\|S_{B}(t)f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}^{2},

where we use the following interpolation

\langle R\rangle^{\gamma}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a_{0},b}}^{2}\leq\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a_{0},b}}^{2}+\langle R\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-(a-a_{0})\langle R\rangle^{b}}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}^{2},

we can deduce

\displaystyle\|S_{B}(t)f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}\lesssim

\displaystyle e^{-c\langle R\rangle^{\gamma}t}\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}+e^{-(a-a_{0})\langle R\rangle^{b}}\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\inf_{R>0}(e^{-c\langle R\rangle^{\gamma}t}+e^{-(a-a_{0})\langle R\rangle^{b}})\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t^{\frac{b}{b-\gamma}}}\|f\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}},

for some constant
\lambda>0
, where we choose
\langle R\rangle=t^{\frac{1}{b-\gamma}}
. Using Duhamel’s formula again we have

\displaystyle\|(I-P)S_{L}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a_{0},b}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\|(I-P)S_{L}(t-s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-3/4})\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-1/2})}\|A\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a_{0},b}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}(\mu^{-3/4})}\|S_{B}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a_{0},b}}ds

\displaystyle+\|(I-P)S_{B}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}\to L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a_{0},b}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t^{\frac{b}{b-\gamma}}},

so the proof is thus finished.
∎

4.
Estimates for the inhomogeneous equation

In this section we prove the estimates for the inhomogeneous Boltzmann equation.
Recall
N
is defined in (
8
),
w(\alpha,\beta)
is defined in (
10
),
X_{k},X_{k,a,b}
is defined in (
9
) and (
11
), and
Y_{k}:=X_{k+\gamma/2},Y_{k,a,b}:=X_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}
. We first prove an estimate for the nonlinear term.

Lemma 4.1
.

Suppose
f,g,h
smooth function. For the polynomial weight case, for any
k\geq 4
large, if
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
, then for any indices
|\alpha|\leq 2
, we have

|(\partial^{\alpha}Q(f,g),\partial^{\alpha}h\langle v\rangle^{2k})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|\leq C_{k}\|f\|_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{4}}\|g\|_{Y_{k}}\|h\|_{Y_{k}}+C_{k}\|g\|_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{4}}\|f\|_{Y_{k}}\|h\|_{Y_{k}},

for some constant
C_{k}>0
. If
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
, for any indices
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
we have

|(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}Q(f,g),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|\leq C_{k}\|f\langle v\rangle^{4}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\|g\|_{Y_{k}}\|h\|_{Y_{k}}+C_{k}\|g\langle v\rangle^{4}\|_{H^{N}_{x,v}}\|f\|_{Y_{k}}\|h\|_{Y_{k}},

for some constant
C_{k}>0
. For the exponential weight case, for any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},a>0,b\in(0,2)
, if
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
, then for any indices
|\alpha|\leq 2
, we have

|(\partial^{\alpha}Q(f,g),\partial^{\alpha}h\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|\leq C_{k}\|f\|_{X_{k,a,b}}\|g\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}\|h\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}+C_{k}\|g\|_{X_{k,a,b}}\|f\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}\|h\|_{Y_{k,a,b}},

for some constant
C_{k,a,b}>0
. If
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
for any indices
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
we still have

|(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}Q(f,g),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|\leq C_{k,a,b}\|f\|_{X_{k,a,b}}\|g\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}\|h\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}+C_{k,a,b}\|g\|_{X_{k,a,b}}\|f\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}\|h\|_{Y_{k,a,b}},

for some constant
C_{k,a,b}>0
.

Proof.

We first prove the polynomial case, for the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
, by

(\partial^{\alpha}Q(f,g),\partial^{\alpha}h\langle v\rangle^{2k})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}=\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha}(Q(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}f,\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}g),\partial^{\alpha}h\langle v\rangle^{2k})_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

together with Lemma
3.5
we have

\displaystyle(Q(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}f,\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}g),\partial^{\alpha}h\langle v\rangle^{2k})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f\|_{L^{2}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}+\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g\|_{L^{2}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}dx.

By symmetry we only estimate the first term, we easily compute

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f\|_{L^{2}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}dx\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f\|_{H^{2-|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|}_{x}L^{2}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g\|_{H^{|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}h\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|f\|_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{4}}\|g\|_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}}\|h\|_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2}},

so the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
is proved. We then prove the case
(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
, we only prove the case
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{5}{2}]
, the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{5}{2},-\frac{3}{2}]
can be proved similarly. First we have

(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}Q(f,g),\partial^{\alpha}hw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}=\sum_{\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha,\beta_{1}\leq\beta}(Q(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f,\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}g),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

together with Lemma
3.5
we have

\displaystyle(Q(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f,\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}g),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\min_{m+n=2}\{\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{H^{m}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{n}_{\gamma/2}}\}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}dx

\displaystyle+C_{k}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\min_{m+n=2}\{\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g\|_{H^{n}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{m}_{\gamma/2}}\}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}dx.

By symmetry we only need to prove that

\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\min_{m+n=2}\{\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{H^{m}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{n}_{\gamma/2}}\}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}dx\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{4}f\|_{H^{4}_{x,v}}\|g\|_{Y_{k}}\|h\|_{Y_{k}},\quad\forall\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha,\beta_{1}\leq\beta.

First we split it into three cases,
|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|+|\beta-\beta_{1}|\leq 2
,
|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|+|\beta-\beta_{1}|=3
,
|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|+|\beta-\beta_{1}|=4
. For the case
|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|+|\beta-\beta_{1}|\leq 2
, take
m=2,n=0
we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\min_{m+n=2}\{\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{H^{m}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{n}_{\gamma/2}}\}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{H^{c}_{x}H^{2}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{d}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}},

with
c,d
nonnegative integers satisfying
c+d=2
. Taking

c=2-|\beta-\beta_{1}|-|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|,\quad d=|\beta-\beta_{1}|+|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|,

such that

|\beta-\beta_{1}|+|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|+2+c=4,\quad|\alpha_{1}|+|\beta_{1}|+d=|\alpha|+|\beta|,

so first we have

\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{H^{2}_{x}H^{2}_{4}}\leq\|\langle v\rangle^{4}f\|_{H^{4}_{x,v}},\quad\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\leq\|h\|_{Y_{k}}.

Using the fact that

|\alpha_{1}|+|\beta_{1}|=|\alpha|+|\beta|\quad\&\quad|\beta_{1}|\leq|\beta|\quad\Rightarrow\quad w(\alpha,\beta)\leq w(\alpha_{1},\beta_{1}),

we deduce

\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{d}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}=\sum_{|d|\leq|\beta-\beta_{1}|+|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{d}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\leq\|g\|_{Y_{k}}.

For the case
|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|+|\beta-\beta_{1}|=3
, by
\|fg\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq\|f\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|g\|_{L^{3}_{x}}

and taking
m=0,n=2
we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\min_{m+n=2}\{\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{H^{m}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{n}_{\gamma/2}}\}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{3}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{3}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}.

Since
|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|+|\beta-\beta_{1}|=3
, we have

\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{H^{1}_{x}H^{a}_{4}}\leq\|\langle v\rangle^{4}f\|_{H^{4}_{x,v}},\quad\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\leq\|h\|_{Y_{k}}.

For the
g
term, since
|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq 3
, we split it into two cases
|\alpha|+|\beta|=3
and
|\alpha|+|\beta|=4
. For the case
|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|+|\beta-\beta_{1}|=|\alpha|+|\beta|=3
we have
|\alpha_{1}|=|\beta_{1}|=0
, by (
28
)

\max_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=3}w^{2}(\alpha,\beta)\leq w(0,2)w(1,2),

together with (
31
) we have

\|gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{3}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\leq\|gw(0,2)\|_{L^{2}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}+\|gw(1,2)\|_{H^{1}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\lesssim\|g\|_{Y_{k}}.

For the case
|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|+|\beta-\beta_{1}|=3,|\alpha|+|\beta|=4
, this time we have
|\alpha_{1}|=1,|\beta_{1}|=0
or
|\alpha_{1}|=0,|\beta_{1}|=1
. For the first case by (
29
)

\max_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=4}w^{2}(\alpha,\beta)\leq w(1,2)w(2,2),

together with (
31
) we have

\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{3}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\lesssim\|gw(1,2)\|_{H^{1}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}+\|gw(2,2)\|_{H^{2}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\lesssim\|g\|_{Y_{k}}.

For the second case by (
29
)

\max_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=4}w^{2}(\alpha,\beta)\leq w(0,3)w(1,3),

together with (
31
) we deduce

\|\partial_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{3}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\lesssim\|gw(0,3)\|_{L^{2}_{x}H^{3}_{\gamma/2}}+\|gw(1,3)\|_{H^{1}_{x}H^{3}_{\gamma/2}}\lesssim\|g\|_{Y_{k}}.

Finally for
|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|+|\beta-\beta_{1}|=4
, we have
|\alpha_{1}|=|\beta_{1}|=0
, taking
m=0,n=2
we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\min_{m+n=2}\{\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}f\|_{H^{m}_{4}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{n}_{\gamma/2}}\}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{4}}\|gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\langle v\rangle^{4}f\|_{H^{4}_{x,v}}\|gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\|h\|_{Y_{k}},

by (
29
)

\max_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=4}w^{2}(\alpha,\beta)\leq w(1,2)^{4/5}w(2,2)^{6/5},

together with (
30
) we deduce

\|gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\lesssim\|gw(1,2)\|_{H^{1}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}+\|gw(2,2)\|_{H^{2}_{x}H^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\lesssim\|g\|_{Y_{k}},

so the polynomial case is thus proved by gathering all the case. For the exponential weight case, for
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
by Lemma
3.7
we have

\displaystyle(Q(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}f,\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}g),\partial^{\alpha}h\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,a,b}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f\|_{L^{2}_{k,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha}h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}+\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g\|_{L^{2}_{k,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha}h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}dx.

For the first term we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f\|_{L^{2}_{k,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha}h\|_{L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}f\|_{H^{2-|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}g\|_{H^{|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha}h\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|f\|_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k,a,b}}\|g\|_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}\|h\|_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{k,a,b}}\|g\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}\|h\|_{Y_{k,a,b}},

and the second term follows by symmetry. For the case
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
, for simplicity we only prove the case
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{5}{2}]
, by Lemma
3.7
we have

\displaystyle(Q(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}f,\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}g),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,a,b}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\min_{m+n=2}\{\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{m}_{-96,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{n}_{\gamma/2,a,b}}\}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2,a,b}}dx

\displaystyle+C_{k,a,b}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\min_{m+n=2}\{\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{n}_{-96,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{m}_{\gamma/2,a,b}}\}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2,a,b}}dx.

By symmetry we only need to prove that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\min_{m+n=2}\{\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{m}_{-96,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{n}_{\gamma/2,a,b}}\}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}hw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2.a,b}}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|f\|_{X_{k,a,b}}\|g\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}\|h\|_{Y_{k,a,b}},\quad\forall\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha,\beta_{1}\leq\beta.

By (
10
) we have

w(\alpha,\beta)\langle v\rangle^{-96}\leq w(\alpha_{2},\beta_{2}),\quad\forall|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 4,\quad\forall|\alpha_{2}|+|\beta_{2}|\leq 4,

so for any nonnegative integer
m,n
satisfies
m+n+|\alpha-\alpha_{1}|+|\beta-\beta_{1}|\leq 4
we have

\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{H^{m}_{x}H^{n}_{-96,a,b}}\leq\|f\|_{X_{k,a,b}},

the remaining proof is the same as the polynomial case thus omitted, so the proof is thus finished.
∎

Then we come to prove estimate for the linearized part, we first prove the polynomial case.

Lemma 4.2
.

For any smooth function
f
, if
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
, for any indices
|\alpha|\leq 2
we have

\displaystyle|(\partial^{\alpha}Q(f,\mu),\partial^{\alpha}g\langle v\rangle^{2k})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|\leq

\displaystyle\|b(\cos\theta)\sin^{k-2}\frac{\theta}{2}\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,*}}\|\partial^{\alpha}g\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,*}}

\displaystyle+C_{k}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2-1/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}g\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2-1/2}},

for some constant
C_{k}>0
. For the case
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
, for any indices
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
we have

\displaystyle|(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}Q(f,\mu),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|\leq

\displaystyle\|b(\cos\theta)\sin^{k-2}\frac{\theta}{2}\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,*}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,*}}

\displaystyle+C_{k}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2-1/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2-1/2}}

\displaystyle+C_{k}\sum_{\beta_{1}<\beta}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta_{1})\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}.

Proof.

Since
\partial^{\alpha}\mu=0
if
|\alpha|>0
, the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
is just Lemma
3.1
and thus omitted. For the
case
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
, by

(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}Q(f,\mu),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}=\sum_{\beta_{1}\leq\beta}(Q(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}f,\partial_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\mu),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

we split it into two cases. For the case
\beta_{1}=\beta
, by Lemma
3.1
we have

\displaystyle|(Q(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f,\mu),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|\leq

\displaystyle\|b(\cos\theta)\sin^{k-2}\frac{\theta}{2}\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,*}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,*}}

\displaystyle+C_{k}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2-1/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2-1/2}}.

For the case
\beta_{1}<\beta
, by Corollary
3.6
we have

|(Q(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}f,\partial_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\mu),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|\leq C_{k}\sum_{\beta_{1}<\beta}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}},

so the proof is thus finished since
w(\alpha,\beta)\leq w(\alpha,\beta_{1})
if
|\beta_{1}|\leq|\beta|
.
∎

Lemma 4.3
.

For any smooth function
f
, for any
k\geq 4
. If
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
, for any indices
|\alpha|\leq 2
we have

\displaystyle(\partial^{\alpha}Q(\mu,f),\partial^{\alpha}f\langle v\rangle^{2k})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq-\|b(\cos\theta)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,*}}^{2}+C_{k}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2-1/2}}^{2},

for some constant
C_{k}\geq 0
. If
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
, for any indices
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
we have

\displaystyle(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}Q(\mu,f),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq-\|b(\cos\theta)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,*}}^{2}+C_{k}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2-1/2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{k}\sum_{\beta_{1}<\beta}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta_{1})\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}},

for some constant
C_{k}\geq 0
.

Proof.

Since
\partial^{\alpha}\mu=0
if
|\alpha|>0
, the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
is just Lemma
3.2
and thus omitted. For the case
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
, first we have

(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}Q(\mu,f),\partial^{\alpha}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}=\sum_{\beta_{1}\leq\beta}(Q(\partial_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\mu,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}f),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

we split it into two cases. For the case
\beta_{1}=\beta
, by Lemma
3.2
we have

(Q(\mu,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq-\|b(\cos\theta)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,*}}^{2}+C_{k}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2-1/2}}^{2}.

For the case
|\beta_{1}|<|\beta|
, by Corollary
3.6
we have

|(Q(\partial_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\mu,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}f),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|\leq C_{k}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}},

so the lemma is thus proved since
w(\alpha,\beta)\leq w(\alpha,\beta_{1})
if
|\beta_{1}|\leq|\beta|
.
∎

Then we prove the estimate for the exponential weight case.

Lemma 4.4
.

For any smooth function
f
, for any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},a>0,b\in(0,2)
. If
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
, for any indices
|\alpha|\leq 2
we have

(\partial^{\alpha}Q(f,\mu),\partial^{\alpha}f\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+(\partial^{\alpha}Q(\mu,f),\partial^{\alpha}f\langle v\rangle^{2k}e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq-C_{1}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{k+\gamma/2,a,b}}^{2}+C_{k,a,b}\|\partial^{\alpha}f\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},

for some constants
C_{1},C_{k,a,b}>0
. If
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
, for any indices
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
we have

\displaystyle(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}Q(f,\mu),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}Q(\mu,f),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-C_{1}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,a,b}}^{2}+C_{k,a,b}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+C_{k,a,b}\sum_{\beta_{1}<\beta}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta_{1})\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,a,b}},

for some constants
C_{1},C_{k,a,b}>0
.

Proof.

Since
\partial^{\alpha}\mu=0
if
|\alpha|>0
, the case
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
is just Corollary
3.11
and thus omitted. For the case
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
, first we have

\displaystyle(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}Q(f,\mu),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}Q(\mu,f),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\beta_{1}\leq\beta}(Q(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}f,\partial_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\mu),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+(Q(\partial_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\mu,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}f),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

we split it into two cases. For the case
\beta_{1}=\beta
, by Corollary
3.11
we have

\displaystyle(Q(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f,\mu),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+(Q(\mu,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-C_{1}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,a,b}}^{2}+C_{k,a,b}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For the case
\beta_{1}<\beta
, by Corollary
3.8
we have

\displaystyle|(Q(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}f,\partial_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\mu),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|+|(Q(\partial_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\mu,\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}f),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta)e^{2a\langle v\rangle^{b}})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,a,b}\sum_{\beta_{1}<\beta}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,a,b}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2,a,b}},

so the lemma is thus proved since
w(\alpha,\beta)\leq w(\alpha,\beta_{1})
if
\beta_{1}\leq\beta
.
∎

For the transport term
v\cdot\nabla_{x}f
we need the following estimate.

Lemma 4.5
.

Suppose
|\beta|>0
, for any smooth function
f
we have

(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}f),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw^{2}(\alpha,\beta))_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq C(\sum_{|\beta_{2}|=|\beta|-1,|\alpha_{2}|=|\alpha|+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}fw(\alpha_{2},\beta_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}})\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}.

Proof.

By (
27
) and

\partial_{v_{i}}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}f)=\partial_{x_{i}}f+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial_{v_{i}}f,\quad\forall i=1,2,3,

the theorem is thus proved.
∎

Recall the definition of
\bar{X}_{0}
.
\bar{X}_{0}:=H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
if
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
,
\bar{X}_{0}=H^{N}_{x,v}
, if
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
, and
Y_{k,*}
is defined in (
13
). Gathering the estimates above, we obtian following estimate.

Lemma 4.6
.

For any smooth function
f,g,h
smooth, for the polynomial case, for any
k>4
large, for the nonlinear term we have

(34)

|(Q(f,g),h)_{X_{k}}|\lesssim(\|f\|_{X_{4}}\|g\|_{Y_{k}}+\|f\|_{Y_{k}}\|g\|_{X_{4}})\|h\|_{Y_{k}}.

For the linearized term we have

(35)

|(Q(f,\mu),g)_{X_{k}}|\leq\|b(\cos\theta)\sin^{k-2-\frac{k-4}{3}}\frac{\theta}{2}\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}\|f\|_{Y_{k,*}}\|g\|_{Y_{k,*}}+C_{k}\|f\|_{Y_{k-1/2}}\|g\|_{Y_{k-1/2}},

and

(36)

(Q(\mu,f),f)_{X_{k}}\leq-\|b(\cos\theta)\sin^{2+\frac{k-4}{3}}\frac{\theta}{2}\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}\|f\|_{Y_{k,*}}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{Y_{k-1/2}}^{2}.

In particular gathering the two terms we have

(37)

(Q(\mu,f),f)_{X_{k}}+(Q(f,\mu),f)_{X_{k}}\leq-\|b(\cos\theta)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(\sin^{\frac{k-4}{3}}\frac{\theta}{2}-\sin^{\frac{2(k-4)}{3}}\frac{\theta}{2})\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}\|f\|_{Y_{k,*}}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{Y_{k-1/2}}^{2}.

For the exponential weight case, for the nonlinear term we have

(38)

|(Q(f,g),h)_{X_{k,a,b}}|\lesssim(\|f\|_{X_{k,a,b}}\|g\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}+\|f\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}\|g\|_{X_{k,a,b}})\|h\|_{Y_{k,a,b}},

for the linearized term we have

(39)

(Q(\mu,f),f)_{X_{k,a,b}}+(Q(f,\mu),f)_{X_{k,a,b}}\leq-C_{2}\|f\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}.

For the
v\cdot\nabla_{x}f
term, for the polynomial case we have

(40)

|(-v\cdot\nabla_{x}f,f)_{X_{k}}|\leq\frac{1}{4}\|b(\cos\theta)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(\sin^{\frac{k-4}{3}}\frac{\theta}{2}-\sin^{\frac{2(k-4)}{3}}\frac{\theta}{2})\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}\|f\|_{Y_{k,*}}^{2},

for the exponential weight case we have

(41)

|(-v\cdot\nabla_{x}f,f)_{X_{k,a,b}}|\leq\frac{C_{2}}{4}\|f\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}^{2}.

Proof.

(
34
) and (
38
) can be proved by summing on
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
(
|\alpha|\leq 2
if
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
) in Lemma
4.1
. For any
\eta>0
we have

\displaystyle C_{|\alpha|,|\beta|}^{2}C_{k}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|<|\beta|}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta_{1})\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|<|\beta|}\frac{C_{k}C_{|\alpha|,|\beta|}}{\eta}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta_{1}}fw(\alpha,\beta_{1})\|_{L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}\eta C_{|\alpha|,|\beta|}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}gw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{\gamma/2}.

By (
12
) we have
C_{|\alpha|,|\beta|}\ll C_{|\alpha|,|\beta_{1}|}
, so (
35
) follows from summing on
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
(
|\alpha|\leq 2
if
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
) in Lemma
4.2
and taking suitable small constants
\eta>0
such that

\eta\ll\|b(\cos\theta)(\sin^{k-2-\frac{k-4}{3}}\frac{\theta}{2}-\sin^{k-2}\frac{\theta}{2})\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}},\quad\frac{C_{k}C_{|\alpha|,|\beta|}}{\eta}\ll C_{|\alpha|,|\beta_{1}|},

the estimate (
36
) and (
39
) can be proved similarly. For the
v\cdot\nabla_{x}f
term, if
\gamma\in(-\frac{3}{2},1]
, it is easily seen that

(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{\alpha}f,\partial^{\alpha}f\langle v\rangle^{2k})_{L^{2}_{x,v}}=0.

For the case
\gamma\in(-3,-\frac{3}{2}]
we have

\displaystyle C_{|\alpha|,|\beta|}^{2}C_{k}(\sum_{|\beta_{2}|=|\beta|-1,|\alpha_{2}|=|\alpha|+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}fw(\alpha_{2},\beta_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}})\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\frac{C_{|\alpha|,|\beta|}C_{k}}{\eta}\sum_{|\beta_{2}|=|\beta|-1,|\alpha_{2}|=|\alpha|+1}\|\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}fw(\alpha_{2},\beta_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}})\eta C_{|\alpha|,|\beta|}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}fw(\alpha,\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{\gamma/2}}.

By (
12
) we have
C_{|\alpha|,|\beta|}\ll C_{|\alpha|+1,|\beta|-1}
, so (
40
) follows by summing on
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
in Lemma
4.5
and taking suitable
\eta
such that

\frac{C_{|\alpha|,|\beta|}C_{k}}{\eta}\ll C_{|\alpha|+1,|\beta|-1},\quad\eta\ll\frac{1}{2}\|b(\cos\theta)\sin^{2}\frac{\theta}{2}(\sin^{\frac{k-4}{3}}\frac{\theta}{2}-\sin^{\frac{2(k-4)}{3}}\frac{\theta}{2})\|_{L^{1}_{\theta}}.

And (
41
) can be proved similarly.
∎

Taking
g=f
in Lemma
4.6
we can easily obtain the following estimate.

Corollary 4.7
.

Suppose that
-3<\gamma\leq 1
,
f
smooth. For the polynomial case, for any
k>4
large, there exists constants
c_{0},C_{k}>0
such that

\displaystyle(Q(\mu+f,\mu+f),f)_{X_{k}}

\displaystyle\leq-2c_{0}\|f\|_{Y_{k}}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{Y_{k-1/2}}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{\bar{X}_{4}}\|f\|_{Y_{k}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-c_{0}\|f\|_{Y_{k}}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{X_{4}}\|f\|_{Y_{k}}^{2}.

For the exponential weight case, for any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},a>0,b\in(0,2)
we have

(Q(\mu+f,\mu+f),f)_{X_{k,a,b}}\leq-c_{0}\|f\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}^{2}+C_{k,a,b}\|f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}+C_{k,a,b}\|f\|_{X_{k,a,b}}\|f\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}^{2},

for some constants
c_{0},C_{k,a,b}>0
.

Corollary 4.8
.

Suppose
\gamma\in(-3,1]
. For any smooth function
f
, suppose
f
is the solution of

\partial_{t}f=\bar{L}f:=-v\cdot\nabla_{x}f+Q(f,\mu)+Q(\mu,f),\quad f|_{t=0}=f_{0}.

If
\gamma\in[0,1]
, for any
k>4
we have

\|f-Pf\|_{X_{k}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t}\|f_{0}-Pf_{0}\|_{X_{k}},

for some constant
\lambda>0
. If
\gamma\in(-3,0)
, for any
4<k_{0}<k
we have

\|f-Pf\|_{X_{k_{0}}}\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{k-k_{*}}{|\gamma|}}\|f_{0}-Pf_{0}\|_{X_{k}},\quad\forall k_{*}\in(k_{0},k).

For the exponential weight, if
\gamma\in[0,1]
, for any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},0<a,b\in(0,2)
we have

\|f-Pf\|_{X_{k,a,b}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t}\|f_{0}-Pf_{0}\|_{X_{k,a,b}},

for some constant
\lambda>0
. If
\gamma\in(-3,0)
, for any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},0<a_{0}<a,b\in(0,2)
we have

\|f-Pf\|_{X_{k,a_{0},b}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t^{\frac{b}{b-\gamma}}}\|f_{0}-Pf_{0}\|_{X_{k,a,b}},

for some constant
\lambda>0
.

Proof.

The proof is similar as Lemma
3.12
thus omitted.
∎

Corollary 4.9
.

Denote
Z_{k}=X_{k-\gamma/2}
, then we have

\|Q(f,g)\|_{Z_{k}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{4}}\|g\|_{Y_{k}}+\|f\|_{Y_{k}}\|g\|_{X_{4}}.

Proof.

It’s easily seen that
Z_{k}
is the dual of
Y_{k}
with respect to
X_{k}
, so the corollary follows by (
34
) in Lemma
4.6
.
∎

5.
Global existence and convergence

The proof of local existence is standard once we have established estimates in Lemma
4.6
, we refer to
[
7
]
for example.

Theorem 5.1
.

(Local existence) For any
k>4
, there exists
\epsilon_{0},\epsilon_{1},T>0
such that if
f_{0}\in X_{k}
and

\|f_{0}\|_{X_{k}}\leq\epsilon_{0},\quad\mu+f_{0}\geq 0,

then the Cauchy problem

\partial_{t}f+v\cdot\nabla_{x}f=Q(\mu+f,\mu+f),\quad f|_{t=0}=f_{0}(x,v),

admits a unique weak solution
f\in L^{\infty}([0,T];X_{k})
satisfying

\|f\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T];X_{k})}\leq\epsilon_{1},\quad\mu+f\geq 0,\quad\|f\|_{L^{2}([0,T];Y_{k})}\leq\epsilon_{1}.

Theorem 5.2
.

Recall
\bar{L}f=-v\cdot\nabla_{x}f+Q(f,\mu)+Q(\mu,f)
. For any function
f
satisfies
Pf=0
, for any
k\geq 6
, define the norm
|||f|||_{X_{k}}
and the associate scalar product
((f,g))_{X_{k}}
by

|||f|||_{X_{k}}^{2}=\eta\|f\|_{X_{k}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\infty}\|S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}d\tau,\quad((f,g))_{X_{k}}=\eta(f,g)_{X_{k}}+\int_{0}^{\infty}(S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f,S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)g)_{\bar{X_{0}}}d\tau.

Similarly for any
k\in{\mathbb{R}},a>0,b\in(0,2)
, define the norm
|||f|||_{X_{k,a,b}}
and the associate scalar product
((f,g))_{X_{k,a,b}}
by

|||f|||_{X_{k,a,b}}^{2}=\eta\|f\|_{X_{k,a,b}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\infty}\|S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}d\tau,\quad((f,g))_{X_{k,a,b}}=\eta(f,g)_{X_{k,a,b}}+\int_{0}^{\infty}(S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f,S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)g)_{\bar{X_{0}}}d\tau.

Then there exists some
\eta>0
, such that the norm
|||\cdot|||_{X_{k}}

(|||\cdot|||_{X_{k,a,b}})
is equivalent to
\|\cdot\|_{X_{k}}

(\|\cdot\|_{X_{k,a,b}})
on the space
\{f\in X_{k}|Pf=0\}

(\{f\in X_{k,a,b}|Pf=0\})
. Moreover there exists some constants
C,K>0
such that any smooth solution to the following equation

(42)

\partial_{t}f=\bar{L}f+Q(f,f),\quad f(0)=f_{0},\quad Pf_{0}=0,

satisfies for the polynomial case

(43)

\frac{d}{dt}|||f|||_{X_{k}}^{2}\leq(C|||f|||_{X_{6}}-K)\|f\|_{Y_{k}}^{2},

and for the exponential weight case

(44)

\frac{d}{dt}|||f|||_{X_{k,a,b}}^{2}\leq(C|||f|||_{X_{k,a,b}}-K)\|f\|_{Y_{k,a,b}}^{2}.

As a consequence, if
|||f_{0}|||_{X_{6}}\leq\frac{K}{2C}
, then there exists a global solution
f\in L^{\infty}([0,\infty),X_{6}),\mu+f\geq 0
to the Boltzmann equation (
42
). Moreover for any
k\geq 6
, if we assume
\|f_{0}\|_{X_{k}}<+\infty
, then for the case
\gamma\in[0,1]
we have

|||f|||_{X_{k}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t}|||f|||_{X_{k}},

for some constant
\lambda>0
. For the case
\gamma\in(-3,0)
, for any
6\leq k_{1}<k
we have

|||f|||_{X_{k_{1}}}\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{\frac{k-k_{*}}{|\gamma|}}|||f|||_{X_{k}},\quad\forall k_{*}\in(k_{1},k).

For the exponential weight case if we assume
|||f_{0}|||_{X_{k,a,b}}\leq\frac{K}{2C}
, then there exists a global solution
f\in L^{\infty}([0,\infty),X_{k,a,b})
,
\mu+f\geq 0
to the Boltzmann equation (
42
). Moreover for the case
\gamma\in[0,1]

|||f|||_{X_{k,a,b}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t}|||f|||_{X_{k,a,b}},

for some constant
\lambda>0
. For the case
\gamma\in(-3,0)
, for any
0<a_{0}<a
we have

|||f|||_{X_{k,a_{0},b}}\lesssim e^{-\lambda t^{\frac{b}{b-\gamma}}}|||f|||_{X_{k,a,b}},

for some constant
\lambda>0
.

Proof.

During the proof, we will denote
X=X_{k},Y=Y_{k}
for the polynomial weight case and
X=X_{k,a,b},Y=Y_{k,a,b}
for the exponential weight case. Since
k\geq 6
for the polynomial weight case, by Corollary
4.8
, for both cases we have

\|S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}\leq\theta(\tau)\|f\|_{X},\quad\lim_{\tau\to\infty}\theta(\tau)=0,\quad\int_{0}^{\infty}\theta^{2}(\tau)d\tau<+\infty,

for some function
\theta(\tau)
, which implies

\int_{0}^{\infty}\|S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}d\tau\lesssim\|f\|_{X}^{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\theta^{2}(\tau)d\tau,

the equivalence between two norms is thus proved. Then we compute

\frac{d}{dt}\frac{1}{2}|||f(t)|||^{2}_{X}=\eta(Q(\mu+f,\mu+f),f)_{X}+\int_{0}^{\infty}(S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)\bar{L}f,S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f)_{\bar{X_{0}}}d\tau+\int_{0}^{\infty}(S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)Q(f,f),S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f)_{\bar{X_{0}}}d\tau.

We will estimate the terms separately, first by Corollary
4.7
we have for the polynomial weight case

(Q(\mu+f,\mu+f),f)_{X}\leq-c_{0}\|f\|_{Y}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{{X_{4}}}\|f\|_{Y}^{2},

and for the exponential weight case

(Q(\mu+f,\mu+f),f)_{X}\leq-c_{0}\|f\|_{Y}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}+C_{k}\|f\|_{{X}}\|f\|_{Y}^{2}.

Recall that

\|S_{L}(\tau)f(t)\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}\leq\theta(\tau+t)\|f_{0}\|_{X},\quad\lim_{\tau\to\infty}\theta(\tau+t)=0,\quad\forall t\geq 0.

For the second term we have

\int_{0}^{\infty}(S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)\bar{L}f,S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f)_{\bar{X_{0}}}d\tau=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{d}{d\tau}\|S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f(t)\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}d\tau=\lim_{\tau\to\infty}\|S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f(t)\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}-\|f(t)\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}=-\|f(t)\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}^{2}.

For the last term we have

\int_{0}^{\infty}(S_{L}(\tau)Q(f,f),S_{L}(\tau)f)_{\bar{X_{0}}}d\tau\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}\|S_{L}(\tau)Q(f,f)\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}\|S_{L}(\tau)f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}d\tau.

For the case
\gamma\in[0,1]
, by Corollary
4.8
and Corollary
4.9
we have

\int_{0}^{\infty}\|S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)Q(f,f)\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}\|S_{L}(\tau)f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}d\tau\lesssim\|Q(f,f)\|_{{X_{5}}}\|f\|_{X_{5}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\lambda\tau}d\tau\lesssim\|Q(f,f)\|_{{Z_{6}}}\|f\|_{Y_{6}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{6}}\|f\|_{Y_{6}}^{2}.

For the case
\gamma\in(-3,0)
, since
6>k_{1}+\frac{|\gamma|}{2}
for some
k_{1}>4
, by Corollary
4.8
and Corollary
4.9
so we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\|S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)Q(f,f)\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}\|S_{\bar{L}}(\tau)f\|_{\bar{X_{0}}}d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\|Q(f,f)\|_{{X_{6+|\gamma|/2}}}\|f\|_{X_{6-|\gamma|/2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{6+|\gamma|/2-k_{1}+6-|\gamma|/2-k_{1}}{|\gamma|}}d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\|Q(f,f)\|_{{Z_{6}}}\|f\|_{Y_{6}}\lesssim\|f\|_{X_{6}}\|f\|_{Y_{6}}^{2},

by taking a suitable
\eta
and combining all the terms, (
43
) and (
44
) is thus proved. For the global existence and convergence rate, if
|||f_{0}|||_{X_{6}}\leq\frac{K}{2C}
, then

\frac{d}{dt}|||f|||_{X_{6}}^{2}\leq(C|||f|||_{X_{6}}-K)\|f\|_{Y_{6}}^{2},

we deduce that
|||f|||_{X_{6}}
is decreasing over time for all
t\geq 0
. Together with the local existence we know that there exists a global solution
f\in L^{\infty}((0,\infty),X_{6})
. Now we come to prove the convergence rate, for the polynomial case, for all
k\geq 6
we have

\frac{d}{dt}|||f|||_{X_{k}}^{2}\leq(C|||f|||_{X_{6}}-K)\|f\|_{Y_{k}}^{2}\leq-\frac{K}{2}\|f\|_{Y_{k}}^{2}.

Thus the convergence rate can be proved similarly as Lemma
3.12
, the exponential weight case can be proved similarly.
∎

6.
Global existence for the Boltzmann equation with large amplitude initial data

In this section we prove the global existence for the Boltzmann equation with large amplitude initial data. We first prove some useful lemmas.

Lemma 6.1
.

For any
\gamma\in(-3,1],k>\max\{3+\gamma,3\},\epsilon>0
small enough we have

\int_{\{|v-v_{*}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\cup|v-v_{*}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}\leq C_{k,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma},\quad\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}C_{k,\epsilon}=0,

for any
v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
.

Proof.

If
|v|\leq\frac{1}{2}
, then
|v_{*}|+\frac{1}{2}\leq 1+|v-v_{*}|
, so we have

\displaystyle\int_{\{|v-v_{*}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\cup|v-v_{*}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}=

\displaystyle\int_{\{|v_{*}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\cup|v_{*}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}}|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v-v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\int_{\{|v_{*}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\cup|v_{*}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}}|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}.

We easily compute that

C_{k}\int_{\{|v_{*}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}}|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}\leq C_{k}\int_{\{|v_{*}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}}|v_{*}|^{\gamma}dv_{*}\leq C_{k}\epsilon^{\gamma+3}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma+3}\leq C_{k}\epsilon^{\gamma+3}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma},

and

C_{k}\int_{\{|v_{*}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\}}|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}\leq C_{k}\int_{\{|v_{*}|>\frac{1}{\epsilon}\}}|v_{*}|^{\gamma-k}dv_{*}\leq C_{k}\epsilon^{k-\gamma-3}\leq C_{k}\epsilon^{k-\gamma-3}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma},

so the case
|v|\leq\frac{1}{2}
is thus proved. Consider now
|v|>1/2
, we split the integral into two regions
|v-v_{*}|>\langle v\rangle/\epsilon
and
|v-v_{*}|\leq\epsilon\langle v\rangle
. For the first region, since
|v|\leq\frac{1}{2}|v_{*}|
implies
|v-v_{*}|\geq\frac{1}{2}|v_{*}|
, so we have

\displaystyle\int_{\{|v-v_{*}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}=\int_{\{|v_{*}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\}}|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v-v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\int_{\{|v_{*}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\}}|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}\leq C_{k}\int_{\{|v_{*}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\}}|v_{*}|^{-k+\gamma}dv_{*}\leq C_{k}\epsilon^{k-\gamma-3}\langle v\rangle^{-k+\gamma+3}\leq C_{k}\epsilon^{k-\gamma-3}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}.

For the second region, since
|v|>1/2
and
|v-v_{*}|\leq\epsilon\langle v\rangle
imply
|v_{*}|\geq\langle v\rangle/4
, hence

\int_{|v-v_{*}|\leq\epsilon\langle v\rangle}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}\leq C_{k}\langle v\rangle^{-k}\int_{|v-v_{*}|\leq\epsilon\langle v\rangle}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}dv_{*}\leq C_{k}\epsilon^{3+\gamma}\langle v\rangle^{-k+\gamma+3},

so the theorem is thus proved by gathering all the terms together.
∎

For the linearized part of the polynomial case we are able to prove a better estimate.

Lemma 6.2
.

For any
-3<\gamma\leq 1
, for any constant
k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
we have

I:=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}dv_{*}d\sigma\leq\frac{c}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{4}}}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}+C_{k}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma-2},

for some constant
c>0
(independent of k) and for all
v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
. Moreover for any
\epsilon>0
small we have

J=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}1_{\{|v-v^{\prime}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\cup|v-v^{\prime}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}dv_{*}d\sigma\leq C_{k,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma},\quad\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}C_{k,\epsilon}=0.

We also have

K:=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{-2}dv_{*}d\sigma\leq C_{k}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma-2}.

Proof.

Since
\gamma-1\leq 0
, by Lemma
2.10
we have

\displaystyle I=

\displaystyle 4\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}\int_{\{\omega:\omega\cdot(v^{\prime}-v)=0\}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{|v^{\prime}-v|^{2}+|w|^{2}}}(\sqrt{|v^{\prime}-v|^{2}+|w|^{2}})^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{|v+w|^{2}}{2}}dwdv^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}\int_{\{\omega:\omega\cdot(v^{\prime}-v)=0\}}|w|^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}e^{-\frac{|v+w|^{2}}{2}}dwdv^{\prime}.

Recall the decomposition (
24
) we have

I\leq 4\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}e^{-\frac{|v_{\perp}|^{2}}{2}}\int_{\{\omega:\omega\cdot(v^{\prime}-v)=0\}}|w|^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}e^{-\frac{|v_{\parallel}+w|^{2}}{2}}dwdv^{\prime}.

Since
\frac{\gamma-1}{2}>-2
, we have

\int_{\{\omega:\omega\cdot(v^{\prime}-v)=0\}}|w|^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}e^{-\frac{|v_{\parallel}+w|^{2}}{2}}dw=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}|w-v_{\parallel}|^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}e^{-\frac{|w|^{2}}{2}}dw\leq C\langle v_{\parallel}\rangle^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}},

hence

I\leq C\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{\gamma-3}{2}}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}e^{-\frac{|v_{\perp}|^{2}}{2}}\langle v_{\parallel}\rangle^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}dv^{\prime}.

Similarly we have

J\leq C\int_{\{|v-v^{\prime}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\cup|v-v^{\prime}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{\gamma-3}{2}}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}e^{-\frac{|v_{\perp}|^{2}}{2}}\langle v_{\parallel}\rangle^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}dv^{\prime}.

We split into two regions
|v|\leq 1
and
|v|>1
. If
|v|\leq 1
, since
\frac{\gamma-3}{2}>-3
, hence

I\leq C_{k}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{\gamma-3}{2}}\frac{1}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}dv^{\prime}\leq C_{k}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma-3}{2}}\leq C_{k}\langle v\rangle^{-100}.

Similarly by Lemma
6.1
we have

J\leq C_{k}\int_{\{|v-v^{\prime}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\cup|v-v^{\prime}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{\gamma-3}{2}}\frac{1}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}dv^{\prime}\leq C_{k,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}.

For the case
|v|>1
, since
|v_{\perp}|\leq|v|
, we split it into two cases
|v_{\perp}|>\frac{|v|}{k}
and
|v_{\perp}|\leq\frac{|v|}{k}
. For the case
|v_{\perp}|>\frac{|v|}{k}
we have

I\leq C_{k}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{\gamma-3}{2}}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}e^{-\frac{|v|^{2}}{2k^{2}}}dv^{\prime}\leq C_{k}e^{-\frac{|v|^{2}}{4k^{2}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{\gamma-3}{2}}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{-k}dv^{\prime}\leq C_{k}\langle v\rangle^{-100},

and by Lemma
6.1

J\leq C_{k}e^{-\frac{|v|^{2}}{4k^{2}}}\int_{\{|v-v^{\prime}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\cup|v-v^{\prime}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{\gamma-3}{2}}\frac{1}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}dv^{\prime}\leq C_{k,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}.

For the case
|v_{\perp}|\leq\frac{|v|}{k}
,
|v|>1
we have

|v^{\prime}|^{2}=|v-v^{\prime}|^{2}+|v|^{2}+2v\cdot(v^{\prime}-v)\geq|v-v^{\prime}|^{2}+|v|^{2}-2|v-v^{\prime}||v_{\perp}|\geq(1-\frac{1}{k})(|v-v^{\prime}|^{2}+|v|^{2}),

and since

(1+\frac{1}{k})^{k}\leq e,\quad|v_{\parallel}|^{2}\geq|v|^{2}-\frac{1}{k^{2}}|v|^{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}|v|^{2},

we deduce

\displaystyle I\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{\gamma-3}{2}}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}e^{-\frac{|v_{\perp}|^{2}}{2}}\langle v_{\parallel}\rangle^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}dv^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{\gamma-3}{2}}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle|v-v^{\prime}|^{2}+|v|^{2}\rangle^{k}}e^{-\frac{|v_{\perp}|^{2}}{2}}dv^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{\frac{\gamma-3}{2}}\frac{1}{(1+\frac{|v-v^{\prime}|^{2}}{1+|v|^{2}})^{\frac{k}{2}}}e^{-\frac{|v_{\perp}|^{2}}{2}}dv^{\prime}.

If we take the change of variables (
25
) we have

I\leq C2\pi\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma-1}{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{\frac{\gamma+1}{2}}\frac{1}{(1+\frac{r^{2}}{1+|v|^{2}})^{\frac{k}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\pi}e^{-\frac{|v|^{2}\cos^{2}\theta}{2}}\sin\theta drd\theta.

Taking another change of variables (
26
), recall (
5
) we deduce

\displaystyle I\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{1}{|v|}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma+1}\int_{0}^{\infty}x^{\frac{\gamma+1}{2}}\frac{1}{(1+|x|^{2})^{\frac{k}{2}}}\int_{-|v|}^{|v|}e^{-\frac{|y|^{2}}{2}}dydx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{\infty}x^{\frac{\gamma+1}{2}}\frac{1}{(1+|x|^{2})^{\frac{k}{2}}}dx\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}e^{-\frac{|y|^{2}}{2}}dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}\int_{0}^{\infty}z^{\frac{\gamma-1}{4}}\frac{1}{(1+z)^{\frac{k}{2}}}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}k^{-\frac{\gamma+3}{4}},

so the term
I
is estimated. By (
26
) and (
25
) we have
x=\frac{|v-v^{\prime}|}{\langle v\rangle}
, which implies

\{|v-v^{\prime}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\cup|v-v^{\prime}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}=\{x\leq\epsilon\cup x\geq\frac{1}{\epsilon}\},

thus for
J
we have

J\leq C\frac{1}{|v|}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma+1}\left(\int_{0}^{\epsilon}+\int_{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}^{\infty}\right)x^{\frac{\gamma+1}{2}}\frac{1}{(1+|x|^{2})^{\frac{k}{2}}}\int_{-|v|}^{|v|}e^{-\frac{|y|^{2}}{2}}dydx\leq C_{k,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma},

so the proof for
J
is thus finished. For the
K
term since

K=\langle v\rangle^{-2}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k+2}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k+2}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}dv_{*}d\sigma,

the estimate for term
K
just follows by the estimate for term
I
.
∎

We introduce the mild solution to the Boltzmann equation. For any
k\geq 0
, let
f(t,x,v)=\langle v\rangle^{-k}(F(t,x,v)-\mu(v))
in (
1
), then
f
satisfies

(45)

\partial_{t}f+v\cdot\nabla_{v}f+L_{k}f=\Gamma_{k}(f,f),

where

L_{k}f:=\langle v\rangle^{k}Q(\mu,f\langle v\rangle^{-k})+\langle v\rangle^{k}Q(f\langle v\rangle^{-k},\mu),

and

\Gamma^{\pm}_{k}(f,f):=\langle v\rangle^{k}Q^{\pm}(f\langle v\rangle^{-k},f\langle v\rangle^{-k}),\quad\Gamma_{k}(f,f):=\Gamma^{+}_{k}(f,f)-\Gamma^{-}_{k}(f,f).

We also have

L_{k}f=K_{k}f-\nu(v)f,\quad\nu(v):=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\mu(v_{*})dv_{*}d\sigma\ \sim\langle v\rangle^{\gamma},

where
K_{k}:=K_{2,k}-K_{1,k}
is defined as

(K_{1,k}f)(v):=\mu(v)\langle v\rangle^{k}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)f(v_{*})\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}d\sigma,

and

\displaystyle(K_{2,k}f)(v):=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{k}f(v_{*}^{\prime})\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{-k}\mu(v^{\prime})dv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{k}\mu(v_{*}^{\prime})f(v^{\prime})\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\langle v\rangle^{k}\mu(v_{*}^{\prime})f(v^{\prime})\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}d\sigma.

Thus the mild solution of (
45
) is given by

\displaystyle f(t,x,v)=

\displaystyle e^{-\nu(v)t}f_{0}(x-vt,v)+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}(K_{k}f)(s,x-v(t-s),v)ds

(46)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s,x-v(t-s),v)ds.

If we define
l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})
is the kernel with respect to
K_{k}
such that

K_{k}f(v)=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})f(v^{\prime})dv^{\prime}.

For the kernel
l_{k}
we have the following estimate.

Lemma 6.3
.

For any
\gamma\in(-3,1]
, for any
k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
we have

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\leq\frac{c}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{4}}}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}+C_{k}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma-2},\quad\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{-2}dv^{\prime}\leq C_{k}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma-2},

for some constant
C_{k}>0
. Moreover, for
\epsilon>0
small enough, we have

\int_{\{|v-v^{\prime}|>\frac{\langle v\rangle}{\epsilon}\cup|v-v^{\prime}|<\epsilon\langle v\rangle\}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\leq C_{k,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma},\quad\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}C_{k,\epsilon}=0.

Proof.

It is easily seen that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\leq

\displaystyle 2\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}e^{-\frac{1}{2}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}}dv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle+\mu(v)\langle v\rangle^{k}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}d\sigma,

we easily conclude by Lemma
2.9
, Lemma
6.1
and Lemma
6.2
.
∎

For the mild solution
f
, we have the following lemma on local existence.

Lemma 6.4
.

(Local existence) Suppose
\gamma\in(-3,1]
,
F_{0}=\mu+f_{0}\geq 0
. For any
k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
suppose
\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}<+\infty
. Then there exists a positive time

t_{1}=C_{k}(1+\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}})^{-1},

such that the Boltzmann equation (
1
) has a unique mild solution
F=\mu+f\geq 0
in
[0,t_{1}]
satisfies

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t_{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq 2\|\langle v\rangle^{k}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Proof.

The proof is similar to Proposition 2.1 in
[
16
]
thus omitted.
∎

We give an upper bound for the nonlinear term.

Lemma 6.5
.

Let
\gamma\in(-3,1]
, for any
k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
,
\alpha\geq 0
, for any
s\geq 0,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}
, for any smooth function
f
it holds that

|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}\Gamma_{k}^{-}(f,f)(s,y,v)|\leq C\nu(v)\|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}},

similarly

|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}\Gamma_{k}^{+}(f,f)(s,y,v)|\leq C\nu(v)\|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\langle v\rangle f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}},

for some constant
p>1
close to 1 which only depends on
\gamma
(such p is fixed and used later).

Proof.

Fix
p>1
close enough to 1 and
\epsilon>0
small enough such that

(47)

-3<p\gamma<\frac{3}{2},\quad\frac{4(p-1)}{p+1}\leq 1,\quad\frac{p-1}{2p}\leq\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\gamma}{6},\quad-3<p\gamma+\epsilon\gamma\leq-2,\quad\epsilon\frac{2p}{p-1}\leq 1.

For the term
\Gamma_{k}^{-}(f,f)
, we easily compute

\displaystyle|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}\Gamma_{k}^{-}(f,f)(s,y,v)|\leq

\displaystyle C\|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v\rangle^{-k}f(s,y,v_{*})dv_{*}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{p\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}dv_{*}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}f(s,y,v_{*})^{\frac{p}{p-1}}dv_{*}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\nu(v)\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-2k}dv_{*}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}f(s,y,v_{*})^{\frac{2p}{p-1}}dv_{*}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\nu(v)\|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}.

For the term
\Gamma_{k}^{+}(f,f)
, since
\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}\leq C_{\alpha}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{\alpha}+C_{\alpha}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{\alpha}
, we have

\displaystyle|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}\Gamma_{k}^{+}(f,f)(s,y,v)|\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}|f(s,y,v_{*}^{\prime})\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\alpha}f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle+C\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}|\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{\alpha}f(s,y,v_{*}^{\prime})f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv_{*}d\sigma:=I_{1}+I_{2}.

Without loss of generality we only prove
I_{2}
in the following, we have

\displaystyle I_{2}=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}|\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{\alpha}f(s,y,v_{*}^{\prime})f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{p\gamma+\epsilon p}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{pk}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{pk}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{pk}}dvd\sigma\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{-\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|^{\frac{p}{p-1}}dv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1-\frac{1}{p}}.

By Lemma
2.10
, for any function
g
we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{-\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}}|g(v^{\prime})|dv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}|g(v^{\prime})|\int_{\{w:w\cdot(v^{\prime}-v)=0\}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{|v^{\prime}-v|^{2}+|w|^{2}}}(\sqrt{|v^{\prime}-v|^{2}+|w|^{2}})^{-\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}}dwdv^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\frac{1}{|v^{\prime}-v|}|g(v^{\prime})|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\frac{1}{(|v^{\prime}-v|^{2}+|w|^{2})^{\frac{1+\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}}{2}}}dwdv^{\prime}.

By a change of variable
w=|v-v^{\prime}|x
