, and since
1+\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}>1
, the integral is integrable and we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{-\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}}|g(v^{\prime})|dv_{*}d\sigma\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{-\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}}|g(v^{\prime})|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\frac{1}{(1+|x|^{2})^{\frac{1+\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}}{2}}}dxdv^{\prime}

(48)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v^{\prime}-v|^{-\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}}|g(v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}.

So using (
6
) and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{-\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|^{\frac{p}{p-1}}dv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v-v^{\prime}|^{-\epsilon\frac{2p}{p-1}}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{-4}dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{4}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|^{\frac{2p}{p-1}}dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\langle v\rangle^{-\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{4}|f|^{\frac{p+1}{p-1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1/2}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\langle v\rangle^{-\epsilon\frac{p}{p-1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{4(p-1)}{p+1}}|f|\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2(p-1)}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

By Lemma
2.11
we have

\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{p\gamma+\epsilon p}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{pk}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{pk}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{pk}}dvd\sigma\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\lesssim\langle v\rangle^{\gamma+\epsilon}.

Gathering the terms two we have

I_{2}\leq C\langle v\rangle^{\gamma+\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{-\epsilon}\|\langle v\rangle^{\alpha}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\langle v\rangle f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}},

the proof is thus finished.
∎

For any
\beta\geq 0
, let
h(t,x,v)=\langle v\rangle^{\beta}f(t,x,v)
, where
f
is a solution to (
45
). it is easily seen that
h
satisfies

(49)

\partial_{t}h+v\cdot\nabla_{x}h+\nu(v)h=\Gamma_{k+\beta}(h,h)+K_{k+\beta}h,

with

K_{k+\beta}h(v)=\langle v\rangle^{\beta}K_{k}(\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}h)(v),\quad\Gamma_{k+\beta}(h,h)=\langle v\rangle^{\beta}\Gamma_{k}(\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}h,\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}h)=\langle v\rangle^{\beta}\Gamma_{k}(f,f).

For the kernel of
K_{k+\beta}
we have

K_{k+\beta}f(v)=\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})f(v^{\prime})dv^{\prime},\quad l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})=l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})\langle v\rangle^{\beta}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{-\beta},

it is easily seen that
l_{k+\beta}
still satisfies Lemma
6.5
, with
C_{k}
replaced by
C_{k+\beta}=C_{k,\beta}
. The mild solution to (
49
) is given by

\displaystyle h(t,x,v)=

\displaystyle e^{-\nu(v)t}h_{0}(x-vt,v)+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}(K_{k+\beta}h)(s,x-v(t-s),v)ds

(50)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\Gamma_{k+\beta}(h,h)(s,x-v(t-s),v)ds.

For the mild solution
h
we have the following estimate.

Lemma 6.6
.

Suppose
f
and
h
satisfy (
45
) and (
49
). For any
\gamma\in(-3,1]
, there exists a constant
k_{0}>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
such that for any
k\geq k_{0},\beta\geq\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}(\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}+\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+H(F_{0}))

\displaystyle+C_{k,\beta}\sup_{t_{1}\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\left(|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\},

for some constant
C_{k,\beta}\geq 1
, where
t_{1}
is defined in Lemma
6.4
.

Proof.

By (
6
) we have

\displaystyle|h(t,x,v)|\leq

\displaystyle e^{-\nu(v)t}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}|(K_{k+\beta}h)(s,x-v(t-s),v)|ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}|\Gamma_{k+\beta}(h,h)(s,x-v(t-s),v)|ds:=e^{-\nu(v)t}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+J_{2}+J_{3}.

For the
J_{3}
term, since
\beta\geq 1
, by Lemma
6.5
we have

|\Gamma_{k+\beta}(h,h)(s,y,v)|=|\langle v\rangle^{\beta}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s,y,v)|\leq C_{k,\beta}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\},

hence

\displaystyle J_{3}\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

For the
J_{2}
term, denote
\tilde{x}=x-v(t-s)
, we have

J_{2}\leq\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})h(s,\tilde{x},v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}ds,

by (
6
) again we have

\displaystyle J_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})s}|h_{0}(\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}s,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|\Gamma_{k+\beta}(h,h)|(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime})d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k+\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|h(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle J_{21}+J_{22}+J_{23}.

For the
J_{21}
term by Lemma
6.3
we have

J_{21}\leq C_{k,\beta}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\leq C_{k,\beta}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For the
J_{22}
term by Lemma
6.5
we have

\displaystyle J_{22}\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

For term
J_{23}
, we first split it into two parts
|v|\leq N
and
|v|\geq N
for some constant
N>0
large to be fixed later. For the case
|v|\geq N
, by Lemma
6.3
we have

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}\leq\frac{c}{(k+\beta)^{\frac{\gamma+3}{4}}}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}+C_{k,\beta}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma-2},

which implies

\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau\leq\frac{c}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{4}}}+C_{k,\beta}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{-2}.

Since
|v|\geq N
implies
\langle v\rangle^{-2}\leq\frac{1}{N^{2}}
, using Lemma
6.3
again we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}

(51)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{c}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{4}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}+C_{k,\beta}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{-2}dv^{\prime}\leq\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}+C_{k,\beta}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma-2}\leq\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N^{2}}\right),

we deduce

J_{23}\leq\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N^{2}}\right)\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\leq\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N^{2}}\right)\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For the case
|v|\leq N
, since
l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})
is unbounded, by Lemma
6.3
we have for any
N,k,\beta
we can find a bounded compact support function
l_{k,N,\beta}
such that

(52)

l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime}):=l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})1_{\frac{\langle v\rangle}{C_{k,N,\beta}}\leq|v-v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}\langle v\rangle},\quad\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})-l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\leq\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma},\quad\forall v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3},

for some large constant
C_{k,N,\beta}>0
. By

\displaystyle l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k+\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})=

\displaystyle(l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})-l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime}))l_{k+\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})+l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime})(l_{k+\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})-l_{k,N,\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime}))

(53)

\displaystyle+l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k,N,\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime}),

we split
J_{23}
into three terms respectively. For the first term we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|(l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})-l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime}))l_{k+\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|h(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}d\tau\leq\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

the second term can be estimated similarly. For the third term, by (
52
) we have
l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime})
and
l_{k,N,\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})
is supported in

\frac{\langle v\rangle}{C_{k,N,\beta}}\leq|v-v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}\langle v\rangle,\quad\frac{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle}{C_{k,N,\beta}}\leq|v^{\prime}-v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle,

since
|v|\leq N
, which implies
l_{k,N,\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})l_{k,N,\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})
is supported in
|v|\leq N,|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}

for some constant
C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}>0
. We split it into two parts,
\tau\in[s-\lambda,s]
and
\tau\in[0,s-\lambda]
, where
\lambda>0
is a small constant to be fixed later. For the case
\tau\in[s-\lambda,s]
, since
|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k,N,\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|h(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}(1-e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})\lambda})\leq C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For the case
\tau\in[0,s-\lambda]
, first we have

(54)

l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime})\leq C_{k,N,\beta},\quad l_{k,N,\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})\leq C_{k,N,\beta},\quad\frac{1}{C_{k,N,\beta}}\leq\nu(v)\leq C_{k,N,\beta},\quad\frac{1}{C_{k,N,\beta}}\leq\nu(v^{\prime})\leq C_{k,N,\beta},

and by Lemma
2.13
we have

\displaystyle\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}}|h(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime}dv^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}}\frac{|F(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})-\mu(v^{\prime\prime})|}{\langle v^{\prime\prime}\rangle^{k+\beta}}dv^{\prime}dv^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}}\frac{|F(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})-\mu(v^{\prime\prime})|}{\sqrt{\mu(v^{\prime\prime})}}I_{\{|F(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})-\mu(v^{\prime\prime})|\leq\mu(v^{\prime\prime})\}}dv^{\prime}dv^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle+C_{k,N,\beta}\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}}|F(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})-\mu(v^{\prime\prime})|I_{\{|F(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})-\mu(v^{\prime\prime})|\geq\mu(v^{\prime\prime})\}}dv^{\prime}dv^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\frac{1}{(s-\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\int_{|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}}\frac{|F(\tau,y,v^{\prime\prime})-\mu(v^{\prime\prime})|^{2}}{\mu(v^{\prime\prime})}I_{\{|F(\tau,y,v^{\prime\prime})-\mu(v^{\prime\prime})|\leq\mu(v^{\prime\prime})\}}dv^{\prime\prime}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{k,N,\beta}\frac{1}{(s-\tau)^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\int_{|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}}|F(\tau,y,v^{\prime\prime})-\mu(v^{\prime\prime})|I_{\{|F(\tau,y,v^{\prime\prime})-\mu(v^{\prime\prime})|\geq\mu(v^{\prime\prime})\}}dv^{\prime\prime}dy

(55)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\frac{1}{(s-\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+C_{k,N,\beta}\frac{1}{(s-\tau)^{3}}H(F_{0}),

where we have made a change of variable
y=\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau)
. Since
s-\tau\geq\lambda
, so we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k,N,\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s-\lambda}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|h(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau,v^{\prime\prime}))|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-c(t-s)}\int_{0}^{s-\lambda}e^{-c(s-\tau)}\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}}|h(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-3}H(F_{0}).

Gathering all the terms and taking supremum we have

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N}+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda\right)\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-3}H(F_{0})+C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\left(|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

First fix
\beta\geq 0
, then choose
k
large, then let
N
be sufficiently large and finally let
\lambda
be sufficiently small such that

\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N}+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2},

which implies

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq C_{k,\beta}(\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+H(F_{0}))+C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\left(|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\},

using Lemma
6.4
we have

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t_{1},y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\left(|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t_{1}}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\leq C\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2},

so the proof is thus finished.
∎

Lemma 6.7
.

Suppose
\gamma\in(-3,1]
and
k,\beta>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
, then for any smooth function
f
and
h
satisfy (
45
) and (
49
) we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(t,x,v)|dv\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)t}|f_{0}(x-vt,v)|dv+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-3}H(F_{0})

\displaystyle+C_{k,\beta}(\lambda+\frac{1}{N^{\frac{\beta-3}{2}}})(\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2})

\displaystyle+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-3}(\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+H(F_{0}))^{1-\frac{1}{p}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1+\frac{1}{p}},

where
\lambda>0,N\geq 1
are to be chosen later. Recall that
p>1
is defined in (
47
).

Proof.

By (
6
) we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(t,x,v)|dv\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)t}|f_{0}(x-vt,v)|dv+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}|(K_{k}f)(s,x-v(t-s),v)|dvds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}|\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s,x-v(t-s),v)|dvds

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)t}|f_{0}(x-vt,v)|dv+H_{1}+H_{2}.

For the term
H_{1}
, recall

h(t,x,v)=\langle v\rangle^{\beta}f(t,x,v),\quad l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})=l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})\langle v\rangle^{\beta}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{-\beta},

hence

H_{1}\leq\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})h(s,x-v(t-s),v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}dvds.

We split it into two case
s\in[t-\lambda,t]
and
s\in[0,t-\lambda]
, where
\lambda
is a small constant to be fixed later. For the case
s\in[t-\lambda,t]
, since
\beta-\gamma>3
we have

\displaystyle\int_{t-\lambda}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})h(s,x-v(t-s),v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{t-\lambda}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{t-\lambda}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}dvds\leq C_{k,\beta}\lambda\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For the case
s\in[0,t-\lambda]
, we split it into two cases
|v|\geq N
and
|v|\leq N
for some large constant
N
to be fixed later. For the case
|v|\geq N
, since
\beta>3
we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}\int_{|v|\geq N}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})h(s,x-v(t-s),v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{|v|\geq N}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{|v|\geq N}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}dvds\leq C_{k,\beta}\frac{1}{N^{\beta-3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For the case
|v|\leq N
, using decomposition (
52
) we split it into two terms respectively. For the first term since
\beta>3
we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}\int_{|v|\leq N}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})-l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime})||h(s,x-v(t-s),v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\frac{1}{N}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}dvds\leq C_{k,\beta}\frac{1}{N}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For the last term since
|v|\leq N
, by (
54
) and (
6
) we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}\int_{|v|\leq N}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime})||h(s,x-v(t-s),v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}e^{c(t-s)}\int_{|v|\leq N}\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}}|h(s,x-v(t-s),v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}dvds\leq C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-3}H(F_{0}).

Then we come to the
H_{2}
term, we have

\displaystyle|H_{2}|\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}|\Gamma_{k}^{-}(f,f)(s,x-v(t-s),v)|dvds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}|\Gamma_{k}^{+}(f,f)(s,x-v(t-s),v)|dvds:=H_{21}+H_{22}.

For the
H_{21}
term, we split it into four terms for some constant
\lambda,N>0
to be fixed later

\displaystyle H_{21}\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}|f(s,x-v(t-s),v_{*})||f(s,x-v(t-s),v)|dv_{*}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k-\beta}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}|h(s,x-v(t-s),v_{*})|dv_{*}dvds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\left(\int_{t-\lambda}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}+\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}\int_{|v|\geq N}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}+\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{|v_{*}|\geq N}+\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}\int_{|v|\leq N}\int_{|v_{*}|\leq N}\right)\{\cdot\cdot\cdot\}dv_{*}dvds

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}+I_{4}.

For the term
I_{1}
, since
\beta,k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}

I_{1}\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\int_{t-\lambda}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k-\beta}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}dv_{*}dvds\leq C_{k,\beta}\lambda\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For the term
I_{2}
, since
\beta,k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}

I_{2}\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{|v|\geq N}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}dv\leq C_{k,\beta}\frac{1}{N^{\beta-3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For the term
I_{3}
, since
\beta,k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{|v_{*}|\geq N}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k-\beta}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}dv_{*}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{1}{N^{\beta}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{|v_{*}|\geq N}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}dv_{*}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\frac{1}{N^{\beta}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}dv\leq C_{k,\beta}\frac{1}{N^{\beta}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For the
I_{4}
term, since
\beta,k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
, similar as (
6
) we have

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}e^{-c(t-s)}\int_{|v|\leq N}\int_{|v_{*}|\leq N}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k-\beta}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}|h(s,x-v(t-s),v_{*})|dv_{*}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}e^{-c(t-s)}\left(\int_{|v|\leq N}\int_{|v_{*}|\leq N}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma p}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-kp-\beta p}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta p}dv_{*}dv\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{|v|\leq N}\int_{|v_{*}|\leq N}|f(s,x-v(t-s),v_{*})|^{\frac{p}{p-1}}dv_{*}dv\right)^{1-\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1+\frac{1}{p}}\left(\int_{|v|\leq N}\int_{|v_{*}|\leq N}|f(s,x-v(t-s),v_{*})|dv_{*}dv\right)^{1-\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-3}(\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+H(F_{0}))^{1-\frac{1}{p}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1+\frac{1}{p}},

where
p
is defined in (
47
). For the
H_{22}
term, we split it into four terms for some constant
\lambda,N>0
to be fixed later

\displaystyle H_{22}\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}|f(s,x-v(t-s),v_{*}^{\prime})||f(s,x-v(t-s),v^{\prime})|dv_{*}d\sigma dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k+\beta}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k+\beta}}|h(s,x-v(t-s),v_{*}^{\prime})||h(s,x-v(t-s),v^{\prime})|dv_{*}d\sigma dvds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C\left(\int_{t-\lambda}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}+\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}\int_{|v|\geq N}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}+\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{|v_{*}|\geq N}+\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}\int_{|v|\leq N}\int_{|v_{*}|\leq N}\right)\{\cdot\cdot\cdot\}dv_{*}dvds:=I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}+I_{4}.

For the
I_{1}
term, since
\beta,k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
, by Lemma
2.11

I_{1}\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\int_{t-\lambda}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta+\gamma}dvds\leq C_{k,\beta}\lambda\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For the
I_{2}
term, since
\beta,k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
still by Lemma
2.11

I_{2}\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{|v|\geq N}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta}dv\leq C_{k,\beta}\frac{1}{N^{\beta-3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For the
I_{3}
term, since
\beta,k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
, since
\langle v_{*}\rangle\leq\langle v^{\prime}\rangle\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle
, still by Lemma
2.11
apply for
k+\beta-\frac{\beta-3}{2}
we have

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{|v_{*}|\geq N}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k+\beta-\frac{\beta-3}{2}}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k+\beta-\frac{\beta-3}{2}}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{\frac{\beta-3}{2}}}dv_{*}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\frac{1}{N^{\frac{\beta-3}{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{-\beta+\frac{\beta-3}{2}}dv\leq C_{k,\beta}\frac{1}{N^{\frac{\beta-3}{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}.

For the
I_{4}
term, if
\beta,k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
, similar as (
6
) we have

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}e^{-c(t-s)}\int_{|v|\leq N}\int_{|v_{*}|\leq N}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k+\beta}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k+\beta}}|h(s,x-v(t-s),v^{\prime})|dv_{*}dvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}e^{-c(t-s)}\left(\int_{|v|\leq N}\int_{|v_{*}|\leq N}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma p}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{pk}}{\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{pk}\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{pk}}d\sigma dv_{*}dv\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{|v|\leq N}\int_{|v_{*}|\leq N}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|f(s,x-v(t-s),v^{\prime})|^{\frac{p}{p-1}}d\sigma dv_{*}dv\right)^{1-\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t-\lambda}e^{-c(t-s)}\left(\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N}\int_{|v_{*}^{\prime}|\leq 2N}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|f(s,x-v(t-s),v^{\prime})|^{\frac{p}{p-1}}d\sigma dv_{*}^{\prime}dv^{\prime}\right)^{1-\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-3}(\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+H(F_{0}))^{1-\frac{1}{p}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{1+\frac{1}{p}},

since

\{|v|\leq N,|v_{*}|\leq N,\sigma\in\mathbb{S}^{2}\}\quad\subset\quad\{|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N,|v_{*}^{\prime}|\leq 2N,\sigma\in\mathbb{S}^{2}\},

and
p
is defined in (
47
). The theorem is thus proved by gathering all the terms.
∎

Then we come to the proof for the global existence for the Boltzmann equation with large amplitude initial data.

Proof.

(
Proof of Theorem
1.3
) Fix
\beta,k>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
satisfies the assumption in Lemma
6.6
and Lemma
6.7
. By the assumption in Theorem
1.3
we have
\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq M
. We first assume that

(56)

\|h(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 2A_{0}:=2C_{k,\beta}(2M^{2}+\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+H(F_{0})),

where
C_{k,\beta}
is defined in Lemma
6.6
. By Lemma
6.6
and the priori assumption (
56
) we have

(57)

\|h(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq A_{0}+C_{k,\beta}(2A_{0})^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\cdot\sup_{t_{1}\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,\eta)|d\eta\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}.

Since
x\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}
, for any
t\geq t_{1}
we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}e^{-\nu(t)}|f_{0}(x-vt,v)|dv\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{|v|\geq\lambda}+\int_{|v|\leq\lambda}\right)|f_{0}(x-vt,v)|dv

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|w_{\beta}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3}{\beta}}\|f_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}^{1-\frac{3}{\beta}}+\frac{C}{t_{1}^{3}}\|f_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CM^{\frac{3}{\beta}}\|f_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}^{1-\frac{3}{\beta}}+CM^{3}\|f_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}},

where we have chosen
\lambda=\|w_{\beta}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{1}{\beta}}\|f_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}^{-\frac{1}{\beta}}
and
t_{1}
is defined in Lemma
6.4
. By Lemma
6.7
and the priori assumption (
56
) we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t_{1}\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,\eta)|d\eta\leq

\displaystyle CM^{3}\|f_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}+CM^{\frac{3}{\beta}}\|f_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}^{1-\frac{3}{\beta}}+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-3}H(F_{0})

\displaystyle+C_{k,\beta}(\lambda+\frac{1}{N^{\frac{\beta-3}{2}}})(2A_{0})^{2}+C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-3}(\sqrt{H(F_{0})}+H(F_{0}))^{1-\frac{1}{p}}(2A_{0})^{1+\frac{1}{p}}.

First choose
N
large then choose
\lambda
small, finally choose
H(F_{0})
and
\|f_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}
small we deduce

(58)

4C_{k,\beta}A_{0}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\sup_{t_{1}\leq s\leq t}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,\eta)|d\eta\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\leq\frac{1}{2},

together with (
57
) implies that

\|h(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq\frac{7}{4}A_{0},\quad\forall t\geq 0,

hence we have closed the priori assumption (
56
), the proof for the global existence is thus finished.
∎

7.
Convergence rate for the Boltzmann equation with large amplitude initial data

In this section, we consider the time-decay estimates for the global solution we obtained in Section
6
. In the whole section we are under the assumption in Theorem
1.3
such that all the results in Section
6
remain true. In this section we will denote
f
the solution to (
45
) and denote
h
the solution to (
49
).

7.1.
Convergence rate for Hard Potentials

In this subsection we consider the decay estimate for hard potential.
We first recall a lemma on the convolution of semigroups.

Lemma 7.1
.

For
\lambda_{2}>\lambda_{1}>0,t>0
. we have

\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda_{1}(t-s)}e^{-\lambda_{2}s}ds=\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda_{1}s}e^{-\lambda_{2}(t-s)}ds=e^{-\lambda_{1}t}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})s}ds\leq\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1}}e^{-\lambda_{1}t}.

For the case
\gamma\geq 0
, we first prove that the linearized equation converges. We consider the following linearized equation

(59)

\xi_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\xi+\nu(v)\xi+K_{k}\xi=0,\quad\xi(0,x,v)=\xi_{0}(x,v).

For the linearized equation (
59
), the corresponding mild solution is

(60)

\xi(t,x,v)=e^{-\nu(v)t}\xi_{0}(x-vt,v)+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}(K_{k}\xi)(s,x-v(t-s),v)ds.

Lemma 7.2
.

There exists a
k_{0}\geq 6
large such that for any
k\geq k_{0},\gamma\in[0,1]
, suppose
\xi(t)
is the solution to the linearized equation (
59
), we have

\|\xi(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq C_{k}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\|\xi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},\quad\forall t\geq 0,

for some constants
C_{k},\lambda_{2}>0
.

Proof.

The proof is similar to Lemma
6.6
. First (
60
) implies

|\xi(t,x,v)|\leq e^{-\nu(v)t}\|\xi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}|(K_{k}\xi)(s,x-v(t-s),v)|ds:=e^{-\nu(v)t}\|\xi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+J_{2}.

For the
J_{2}
term, denote
\tilde{x}=x-v(t-s)
, we have

J_{2}\leq\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})\xi(s,\tilde{x},v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}ds.

by (
60
) again we have

\displaystyle J_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})s}|\xi_{0}(\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}s,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|\xi(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds:=J_{21}+J_{22}.

Denote
\lambda_{2}:=\min\{\lambda_{1},\nu(v)|v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\}
, where
\lambda_{1}
is the exponential convergence rate for the linearized semigroup in
L^{2}
proved in Lemma
3.12
. For the
J_{21}
term we have

(61)

J_{21}\leq C_{k}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\|\xi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\frac{\nu(v)}{2}(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\leq C_{k}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\|\xi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For the
J_{22}
term, if
|v|\geq N
, by (
6
) we have

\displaystyle J_{22}\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\frac{\nu(v)}{2}(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\frac{\nu(v^{\prime})}{2}(s-\tau)}dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k}}{N^{2}}\right)e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}.

For the case
|v|\leq N
, similarly as decomposition (
6
) we have, for any
N>0,k\geq 6
we can find a bounded compact support function
l_{k,N}
such that

(62)

l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime}):=l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})1_{\frac{\langle v\rangle}{C_{k,N}}\leq|v-v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}\langle v\rangle},\quad\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})-l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\leq\frac{C_{k}}{N}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma},\quad\forall v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3},

for some large constant
C_{k,N}>0
. By

(63)

l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})=(l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})-l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime}))l_{k}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})+l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime})(l_{k}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})-l_{k,N}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime}))+l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k,N}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime}),

we split
J
into three terms respectively. For the first term we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|(l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})-l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime}))l_{k}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|\xi(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{k}}{N}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{k}}{N}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\},

and the second term can be estimated similarly. For the last term, since
l_{k,N}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})l_{k,N}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})
is supported in

|v|\leq N,|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime}
for some constant
C_{k,N}^{\prime}>0
. We again split it into two cases,
\tau\in[s-\lambda,s]
and
\tau\in[0,s-\lambda]
, where
\lambda>0
is a small constant to be fixed later. For the case
\tau\in[s-\lambda,s]
, since
|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k,N}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|\xi(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\frac{\nu(v)}{2}(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}e^{-\frac{\nu(v^{\prime})}{2}(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}(1-e^{-\frac{\nu(v^{\prime})}{2}\lambda})\leq C_{k,N}\lambda e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}.

For the case
\tau\in[0,s-\lambda]
since

(64)

l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime})\leq C_{k,N},\quad l_{k,N}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})\leq C_{k,N},\quad\frac{1}{C_{k,N}}\leq\nu(v)\leq C_{k,N},\quad\frac{1}{C_{k,N}}\leq\nu(v^{\prime})\leq C_{k,N},

denote
\xi_{1}(t,x,v)=\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{7}{4}}\xi(t,x,v)
, since
k-\frac{7}{4}>4
, by Lemma
3.12
apply for
\xi_{1}
we have

\displaystyle\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime}}|\xi(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime}dv^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime}}|\xi_{1}(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime}dv^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}\frac{1}{(s-\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\int_{|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime}}|\xi_{1}(\tau,y,v^{\prime\prime})|^{2}dv^{\prime\prime}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}\frac{1}{(s-\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|\xi_{1}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq C_{k,N}\frac{1}{(s-\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\lambda_{1}\tau}\|\xi_{1}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\leq C_{k,N}\frac{1}{(s-\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\lambda_{1}\tau}\|\xi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

where we have made a change of variable
y=\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau)
. Since
s-\tau\geq\lambda
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k,N}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s-\lambda}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|\xi(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau,v^{\prime\prime}))|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda_{2}(t-s)}\int_{0}^{s-\lambda}e^{-\lambda_{2}(s-\tau)}\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime}}|\xi(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}\|\xi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda_{2}(t-s)}\int_{0}^{s-\lambda}e^{-\lambda_{2}(s-\tau)}e^{-\lambda_{1}\tau}d\tau ds\leq C_{k,N}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\|\xi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Gathering all the terms we have

\displaystyle\|\xi(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\lambda}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\|\xi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k}}{N}+C_{k,N}\lambda\right)e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}.

First choose
k
large, then let
N
be sufficiently large and finally let
\lambda
be sufficiently small such that

\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k}}{N}+C_{k,N}\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}.

Multiply both side by
e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}
and taking supremum we have

\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\leq C_{k}\|\xi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\frac{1}{2}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}\tau}\|\xi(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\},

which implies

\|\xi(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq C_{k}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\|\xi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

so the convergence for the linear semigroup is thus proved.
∎

Denote
g(t,x,v)=\langle v\rangle^{-2}h(t,x,v)=\langle v\rangle^{\beta-2}f(t,x,v)
, it is easily seen that
g
satisfies

(65)

\partial_{t}g+v\cdot\nabla_{x}g+\nu(v)g=\Gamma_{k+\beta-2}(g,g)+K_{k+\beta-2}g.

We first prove the rate of convergence for
g
.

Lemma 7.3
.

There exists a
k_{0}\geq 8
large such that for any
\gamma\in[0,1],k\geq k_{0},\beta>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
, suppose
g
is the solution to the nonlinear equation (
65
), we have

\|g(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq C_{k,\beta}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},\quad\forall t\geq 0,

for some constant
C_{k,\beta}>0
, where
\lambda_{2}
is defined in Lemma
7.2
.

Proof.

Denote the solution to the linearized equation

\partial_{t}g+v\cdot\nabla_{x}g+\nu(v)g=K_{k+\beta-2}g,\quad g(0,x,v)=g_{0}(x,v),

by
g(t)=V(t)g_{0}
. By Lemma
7.2
we have

\|V(t)g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq C_{k,\beta}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

By Duhamel’s principle we have

g(t)=V(t)g_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}V(t-s)\{\Gamma_{k+\beta-2}(g,g)(s)\}ds=V(t)g_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}V(t-s)\{\langle v\rangle^{\beta-2}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s)\}ds.

We easily compute

\|g(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq C_{k,\beta}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\|g_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\left\|\int_{0}^{t}V(t-s)\{\langle v\rangle^{\beta-2}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s)\}ds\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For the second term, using Duhamel’s principle again we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}V(t-s)\{\langle v\rangle^{\beta-2}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s)\}ds=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\{\langle v\rangle^{\beta-2}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s)\}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{s}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-\tau)}K_{k+\beta-2}\{V(\tau-s)(\langle v\rangle^{\beta-2}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s))\}d\tau ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\{\langle v\rangle^{\beta-2}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s)\}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\tau}e^{-\nu(v)(t-\tau)}K_{k+\beta-2}\{V(\tau-s)(\langle v\rangle^{\beta-2}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s))\}dsd\tau:=A_{1}+A_{2}.

For the term
A_{1}
, since
\beta-2\geq 1
, by Lemma
6.5
we have

\displaystyle|A_{1}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{k,\beta}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\nu(v)\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{3p+1}{2p}}\sup_{y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C_{k,\beta}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\nu(v)e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}ds

\displaystyle\leq C_{k,\beta}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

For the
A_{2}
term, since
\gamma\leq 1
and
\frac{p+1}{2p}\geq\frac{1}{2}
we have

\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq\|\langle v\rangle^{2}g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}=\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p-1}{2p}},

together with Lemma
6.5
we have

\displaystyle\|V(\tau-s)(\langle v\rangle^{\beta-2}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s))\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}(\tau-s)}\|\langle v\rangle^{\beta-2}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}(\tau-s)}\|\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\sup_{y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ce^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}(\tau-s)}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\sup_{y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}},

which implies

\displaystyle|A_{2}|\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\tau}e^{-\nu(v)(t-\tau)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta-2}(v,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\|V(\tau-s)(\langle v\rangle^{\beta-2}\Gamma(f,f)(s))\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}dsd\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\tau}e^{-\nu(v)(t-\tau)}\nu(v)e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}(\tau-s)}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\sup_{y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}dsd\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{\tau}e^{-\nu(v)(t-\tau)}\nu(v)e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}(\tau-s)}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}dsd\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

Gathering the terms and taking supremum we have

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\|g_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\|g_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t_{1}}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}+C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t_{1}\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}M^{4}+C_{k,\beta}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|g(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\sup_{t_{1}\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\},

together with (
58
) we deduce

e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\|g(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 2C_{k,\beta}M^{4},

so the lemma is thus proved.
∎

Proof.

(
Proof of Theorem
1.3
)
Finally we come to prove the rate of convergence for
h
. By (
6
) we have

\displaystyle|h(t,x,v)|\leq

\displaystyle e^{-\nu(v)t}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}|(K_{k+\beta}h)(s,x-v(t-s),v)|ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}|\Gamma_{k+\beta}(h,h)(s,x-v(t-s),v)|ds:=e^{-\nu(v)t}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+J_{2}+J_{3}.

For the
J_{3}
term by Lemma
6.5
we have

\displaystyle J_{3}\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\sup_{y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

For the
J_{2}
term, by (
6
) again we have

\displaystyle J_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})s}|h_{0}(\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}s,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|\Gamma_{k+\beta}(h,h)|(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime})d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k+\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|h(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle:=J_{21}+J_{22}+J_{23}.

For the
J_{21}
term, similarly as (
61
)

J_{21}\leq C_{k}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\frac{\nu(v)}{2}(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\leq C_{k}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{2}t}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For the
J_{22}
term by Lemma
6.5
we have

\displaystyle J_{22}\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}\tau}

\displaystyle\times\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

For the term
J_{23}
, we split into two case
|v|\geq N
and
|v|\leq N
, for the case
|v|\geq N
, by (
6
) we have

\displaystyle J_{23}\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k+\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}\tau}dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N^{2}}\right)e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}.

For the case
|v|\leq N
, by (
52
) and (
6
) we split
J_{23}
into three terms respectively. For the first term we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|(l_{k+\beta}(v,v^{\prime})-l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime}))l_{k+\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|h(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}\tau}d\tau\leq e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\},

the second term can be estimated similarly. For the third term, we have
l_{k,N,\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})l_{k,N,\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})
is supported in
|v|\leq N,|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime},
for some constant
C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}>0
. By Lemma
7.2
we have

\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}}|h(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime}dv^{\prime\prime}\leq C_{k,N,\beta}\|g(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq C_{k,N,\beta}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}\tau}\|g_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq C_{k,N,\beta}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}\tau}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

which implies

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k,N,\beta}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k,N,\beta}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|h(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau,v^{\prime\prime}))|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda_{2}(t-s)}\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\lambda_{2}(s-\tau)}\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N,\beta}^{\prime}}|h(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda_{2}(t-s)}\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\lambda_{2}(s-\tau)}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}\tau}d\tau ds\leq C_{k,N,\beta}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Gathering all the terms we have

\displaystyle\|h(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta,N}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\|h_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N}\right)e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}

\displaystyle+C_{k,\beta}e^{-\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}s}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

Taking suitable
k,\beta,N>0
such that

\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k,\beta}}{N}\leq\frac{1}{4},

together with (
58
) we conclude that

e^{\frac{\lambda_{2}}{4}t}\|h(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 4C_{k,\beta}M^{4},

for some constant
C_{k,\beta}>0
. The rate of convergence for
\gamma\in[0,1]
is thus proved.
∎

7.2.
Convergence rate for the case
-3<\gamma<0

Next we come to prove the convergence for the case
-3<\gamma<0
. Before going to the proof, we first prove some useful lemmas.

Lemma 7.4
.

For any
a,b,k>0
we have

\sup_{0<x\leq b}x^{k}e^{-ax}\leq C_{b,k}(1+a)^{-k},

for some constant
C_{b,k}>0
. As a consequence we have for
-3<\gamma\leq 0
, for all
t>0,k\geq 0

\sup_{v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}\{e^{-\nu(v)t}\nu(v)^{k}\}\leq C(1+t)^{-k},

for some constant
C>0
.

Proof.

Take
f(x)=x^{k}e^{-ax}
, we have

f^{\prime}(x)=kx^{k-1}e^{-ax}-ax^{k}e^{-ax}=x^{k-1}(k-ax)e^{-ax},

so
f^{\prime}(x)=0
when
x=\frac{k}{a}
. We easily deduce that

\sup_{0\leq x\leq b}f(x)=f(\frac{k}{a})=\frac{k^{k}}{a^{k}}e^{-k},\quad\hbox{if}\quad\frac{k}{a}\leq b,\quad\sup_{0\leq x\leq b}f(x)=f(b)=b^{k}e^{-ab},\quad\hbox{if}\quad\frac{k}{a}>b,

so the first statement is thus proved, the second statement just from the fact that
\gamma\leq 0
implies that
0<\nu(v)\leq C
for some constant
C\geq 0
.
∎

Lemma 7.5
.

If
-3<\gamma<0
, then for any
0<r<1
we have

\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\nu(v)(1+s)^{-r}ds\leq C_{r}(1+t)^{-r},\quad\forall v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d},\quad\forall t\geq 0,

for some constant
C_{r}>0
independent of
v
.

Proof.

We split the integral into two parts,
0\leq s\leq\frac{t}{2}
and
\frac{t}{2}\leq s\leq t
. For the first part we have
t-s\geq\frac{t}{2}
, so by Lemma
7.4
we have

\int_{0}^{\frac{t}{2}}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\nu(v)(1+s)^{-r}ds\leq C\int_{0}^{\frac{t}{2}}(1+t-s)^{-1}(1+s)^{-r}ds\leq C(1+t)^{-1}\int_{0}^{\frac{t}{2}}(1+s)^{-r}ds\leq C_{r}(1+t)^{-r}.

For the second part we have
s\geq\frac{t}{2}
, this time we have

\int_{\frac{t}{2}}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\nu(v)(1+s)^{-r}ds\leq C_{r}(1+t)^{-r}\int_{\frac{t}{2}}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\nu(v)ds\leq C_{r}(1+t)^{-r}(1-e^{-\nu(v)\frac{t}{2}})\leq C_{r}(1+t)^{-r},

the proof is thus finished by gathering the two cases.
∎

Remark 7.6
.

If we directly use
(1+t-s)^{-1}
instead of
e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\nu(v)
, we will have

\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-s)^{-1}(1+s)^{-r}ds\leq C_{r}(1+t)^{-r}\log(1+t),

where an extra term
\log(1+t)
occurs.

Lemma 7.7
.

If
\gamma\in(-3,0)
, for any
l\geq 2,k\geq 3
we have

\|\langle v\rangle^{l}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{l}f\|_{L^{2}}^{1+\frac{\gamma}{3}}\|\langle v\rangle^{l}f\|_{L^{\infty}}^{-\frac{\gamma}{3}}\leq C\|f\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle v\rangle^{2l}f\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\|f\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{p-1}{2p}},

where
p
is defined in (
47
).

Proof.

For the
\Gamma_{k}^{+}(f,f)
term we prove by duality, for any smooth function
h
we have

\displaystyle|(\langle v\rangle^{l}\Gamma^{+}_{k}(f,f),h)|\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{k}}{\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{k}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{k}}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*}^{\prime})||f(v^{\prime})||h(v)|\langle v\rangle^{l}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}|f(v^{\prime})|^{2}\langle v\rangle^{2l+\gamma}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{2k}}{\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{2k}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2k}}b(\cos\theta)|h(v)|^{2}\langle v\rangle^{-\gamma}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}.

By Lemma
2.11
we have

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{2k}}{\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\rangle^{2k}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{2k}}b(\cos\theta)|h(v)|^{2}\langle v\rangle^{-\gamma}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\leq\|h\|_{L^{2}}.

Since
2l+\gamma\geq 0
, by pre-post collisional change of variables we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*}^{\prime})|^{2}|f(v^{\prime})|^{2}\langle v\rangle^{2l+\gamma}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*})|^{2}|f(v)|^{2}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{2l+\gamma}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle+C\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*})|^{2}|f(v)|^{2}\langle v\rangle^{2l+\gamma}dvdv_{*}d\sigma:=I_{1}+I_{2}.

Without loss of generality we only compute
I_{1}
, by Lemma
2.5
we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*})|^{2}|f(v)|^{2}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{2l+\gamma}dvdv_{*}d\sigma

(66)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|f\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\left(\sup_{v\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}|f(v_{*})|^{2}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{2l+\gamma}dv_{*}\right)\leq C\|f\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|f^{2}\|_{L^{1}_{2l}}^{1+\frac{\gamma}{3}}\|f^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{2l}}^{-\frac{\gamma}{3}}\leq C\|f\|_{L^{2}_{l}}^{2}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{l}}^{2+\frac{2\gamma}{3}}\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{l}}^{-\frac{2\gamma}{3}},

the
\Gamma^{+}_{k}(f,f)
term is proved by duality. For the
\Gamma_{k}^{-}(f,f)
term, we easily compute

\displaystyle(\langle v\rangle^{l}\Gamma_{k}^{-}(f,f),h)=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)f(v_{*})\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-k}\langle v\rangle^{l}f(v)h(v)dvdv_{*}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b(\cos\theta)|f(v_{*})|^{2}|f(v)|^{2}\langle v\rangle^{2l+\gamma}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-2k}b(\cos\theta)|h(v)|^{2}\langle v\rangle^{-\gamma}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\right)^{1/2}.

The first term is the same as (
7.2
), for the second term we have

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\langle v_{*}\rangle^{-2k}b(\cos\theta)|h(v)|^{2}\langle v\rangle^{-\gamma}dvdv_{*}d\sigma\leq\|h\|_{L^{2}},

so the first inequality is thus proved. For the last inequality by (
47
) we have
\frac{p-1}{2p}\leq\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\gamma}{6}
, together with
l\geq 2
we have

\|\langle v\rangle^{l}f\|_{L^{2}}^{1+\frac{\gamma}{3}}\leq C\|f\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\gamma}{6}}\|\langle v\rangle^{2l}f\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\gamma}{6}}\leq C\|f\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\|\langle v\rangle^{2l}f\|_{L^{\infty}}^{1+\frac{\gamma}{3}-\frac{p-1}{2p}},

the lemma is thus proved.
∎

We first prove that the converge in
L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
, in fact the
L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}
convergence for the linearized semigroup is proved in Lemma
3.12
, so we only need to prove the convergence for the nonlinear equation.

Lemma 7.8
.

Suppose
f
the solution to (
45
), then there exists
k_{0}>\max\{3,3+\gamma\}
such that if
k\geq k_{0}
large,
\beta\geq 6
then we have

\|f(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\leq 4C_{k,\beta}M^{4}(1+t)^{-r_{1}},\quad\forall t\geq 0,

for some constants
C_{k,\beta},r_{1}>1
.

Proof.

Denote the solution to the linearized equation

\partial_{t}\xi+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\xi+\nu(v)\xi=K_{k}\xi,\quad\xi(0,x,v)=\xi_{0}(x,v),

by
\xi(t)=V(t)\xi_{0}
. By Lemma
3.12
we have

\|V(t)\xi\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{l}{|\gamma|}}\|\xi\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{3}},\quad\forall l\in(0,3).

By Duhamel’s principle we have

f(t)=V(t)f_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}V(t-s)\{\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s)\}ds,

which implies

\|f(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\leq C(1+t)^{-r_{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{3}f_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+C\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-s)^{-r_{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{3}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}ds,

for some constant
r_{1}>1
. By Lemma
7.7
we have

\|\langle v\rangle^{3}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\leq C\|f(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{6}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\sup_{y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}},

which implies

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-s)^{-r_{1}}\|\langle v\rangle^{3}\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-s)^{-r_{1}}(1+s)^{-r_{1}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{[(1+s)^{r_{1}}\|f(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}]\|\langle v\rangle^{6}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(1+t)^{-r_{1}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{[(1+s)^{r_{1}}\|f(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}]\|\langle v\rangle^{6}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

Gathering the two terms together we easily have

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(1+s)^{r_{1}}\|f(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}]\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|\langle v\rangle^{3}f_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+C_{k}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{[(1+s)^{r_{1}}\|f(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}]\|\langle v\rangle^{6}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta}M^{4}+C_{k}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(1+s)^{r_{1}}\|f(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}]

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t_{1}\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{6}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

If
\beta\geq 6
, by (
58
) we deduce

\|f(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\leq 2C_{k,\beta}M^{4}(1+t)^{-r_{1}},\quad\forall t\geq 0,

the proof is thus finished.
∎

Proof.

(
Proof of Theorem
1.3
) Recall (
6
), the corresponding mild solution is

\displaystyle|f(t,x,v)|\leq

\displaystyle e^{-\nu(v)t}f_{0}(x-vt,v)+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}|(K_{k}f)(s,x-v(t-s),v)|ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}|\Gamma_{k}(f,f)(s,x-v(t-s),v)|ds:=J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}.

For any
r\in(0,1)
fixed, for the
J_{1}
term by Lemma
7.4
we have

|J_{1}|\leq e^{-\nu(v)t}\nu(v)^{r}|\nu(v)^{-r}f_{0}(x-vt,v)|\leq C(1+t)^{-r}\|\nu^{-r}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq CM(1+t)^{-r}.

For the
J_{3}
term, by Lemma
6.5
we have

\displaystyle J_{3}\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\sup_{y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}(1+s)^{-r}ds\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{[(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}(1+t)^{-r}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{[(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

For the
J_{2}
term, denote
\tilde{x}=x-v(t-s)
we have

J_{2}\leq\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})f(s,\tilde{x},v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}ds.

by (
6
) again we have

\displaystyle J_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})s}|f_{0}(\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}s,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|\Gamma_{k}(f,f)|(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime})d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|f(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds:=J_{21}+J_{22}+J_{23}.

For the
J_{21}
term by Lemma
7.4
and Lemma
7.5
we have

\displaystyle J_{21}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})s}\nu(v^{\prime})^{r}|\nu(v^{\prime})^{-r}f_{0}(\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}s,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nu^{-r}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|(1+s)^{-r}dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|\nu^{-r}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}(1+s)^{-r}dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\|\nu^{-r}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}(1+t)^{-r}\leq C_{k}M(1+t)^{-r}.

For the
J_{22}
term, by Lemma
6.5
we have

\displaystyle J_{22}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|\Gamma_{k}(f,f)|(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime})d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}(1+\tau)^{-r}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k}(1+t)^{-r}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

For the
J_{23}
term, we again split it into two parts
|v|\leq N
and
|v|\geq N
. For the case
|v|\geq N
we have
\langle v\rangle^{-2}\leq\frac{1}{N^{2}}
, by Lemma
6.3
and Lemma
7.5
we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})||f(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|(1+\tau)^{-r}dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})|\left(\frac{c}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{4}}}+C_{k}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{-2}\right)\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}(1+\tau)^{-r}dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k}}{N^{2}}\right)(1+s)^{-r}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\},

which implies

\displaystyle J_{23}\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k}}{N^{2}}\right)\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}(1+s)^{-r}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k}}{N^{2}}\right)(1+t)^{-r}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}.

For the case
|v|\leq N
, by the decomposition (
63
), we split it into three terms respectively.
For the first term by Lemma
7.5
we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|(l_{k}(v,v^{\prime})-l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime}))l_{k}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|f(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{k}}{N}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}(1+\tau)^{-r}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{k}}{N}(1+t)^{-r}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\},

the second term can be estimated similarly. For the last term, since
l_{k,N}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})l_{k,N}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})
is supported in

|v|\leq N,|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime}
for some constant
C_{k,N}^{\prime}>0
. We again split it into two cases,
\tau\in[s-\lambda,s]
and
\tau\in[0,s-\lambda]
, where
\lambda>0
is a small constant to be fixed later. For the case
\tau\in[s-\lambda,s]
, we first prove that

(67)

\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}(1+\tau)^{-r}d\tau\lesssim C_{r}\lambda(1+s)^{-r}.

Similarly as Lemma
7.5
, we split the integral into two cases,
0\leq\tau\leq\frac{s}{2}
and
\frac{s}{2}\leq\tau\leq s
. For the first case we have
s-\tau\geq\frac{s}{2}
, so by Lemma
7.4
we have

\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}(1+\tau)^{-r}d\tau\leq C\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}(1+s-\tau)^{-1}(1+\tau)^{-r}d\tau\leq C(1+s)^{-1}\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}d\tau\leq C_{r}\lambda(1+s)^{-r}.

For the second case we have
\tau\geq\frac{s}{2}
, since
|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime}
, this time we have

\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}(1+\tau)^{-r}d\tau\leq C_{r}(1+s)^{-r}\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}d\tau\leq C_{r}(1+s)^{-r}(1-e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})\lambda})\leq C_{r}\lambda(1+s)^{-r},

so (
67
) is proved by gathering the two cases. Then we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k,N}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|f(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{(1+\tau)^{r}\|f(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}ds\int_{s-\lambda}^{s}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}\langle v^{\prime}\rangle^{\gamma}(1+\tau)^{-r}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}\lambda\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{(1+\tau)^{r}\|f(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}(1+s)^{-r}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}\lambda(1+t)^{-r}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\{(1+\tau)^{r}\|f(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}.

For the case
\tau\in[0,s-\lambda]
, by (
64
) and Lemma
7.8
we have

\displaystyle\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime}}|f(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime}dv^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}\frac{1}{(s-\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\int_{|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime}}|f(\tau,y,v^{\prime\prime})|^{2}dv^{\prime\prime}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C_{k,N}\frac{1}{(s-\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|f(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\leq C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}M^{4}(1+\tau)^{-r},

where we have made a change of variable
y=\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau)
. So we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\nu(v)(t-s)}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|l_{k,N}(v,v^{\prime})l_{k,N}(v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})|\int_{0}^{s-\lambda}e^{-\nu(v^{\prime})(s-\tau)}|f(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau,v^{\prime\prime}))|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda_{2}(t-s)}\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\lambda_{2}(s-\tau)}\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime},|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq C_{k,N}^{\prime}}|f(\tau,\tilde{x}-v^{\prime}(s-\tau),v^{\prime\prime})|dv^{\prime\prime}d\tau dv^{\prime}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}M^{4}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda_{2}(t-s)}\int_{0}^{s}e^{-\lambda_{2}(s-\tau)}(1+\tau)^{-r}d\tau ds\leq C_{k,N,\beta}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}M^{4}(1+t)^{-r}.

Gathering all the terms we have

\displaystyle\|f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta,N}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}M^{4}(1+t)^{-r}+\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k}}{N}+C_{k,N}\lambda\right)(1+t)^{-r}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}

\displaystyle+C_{k}(1+t)^{-r}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\left[(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right]\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{k,\beta,N}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}}M^{4}(1+t)^{-r}+\left(\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k}}{N}+C_{k,N}\lambda\right)(1+t)^{-r}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}

\displaystyle+C_{k}(1+t)^{-r}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(1+s)^{r}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]\sup_{t_{1}\leq s\leq t,y\in{\mathbb{T}}^{3}}\left\{\|h(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\frac{p+1}{2p}}\left(\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}|f(s,y,v^{\prime})|dv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{p-1}{2p}}\right\}.

Taking suitable
k,N,\lambda
such that

\frac{c^{2}}{k^{\frac{\gamma+3}{2}}}+\frac{C_{k}}{N}+C_{k,N}\lambda\leq\frac{1}{4},

together with (
58
) we conclude

\|f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 4C_{k,\beta}M^{4}(1+t)^{-r},\quad\forall t\geq 0.

The rate of convergence is thus proved for the case
-3<\gamma<0
.
∎
